 succeeded in 0ms:
//! Column worlds execute authored observers, including derived/count/refusal paths.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#121-column-read-intent — excluding a field from a projection must not produce a synthetic whole-row refutation.
use super::*;
fn reserve(out:&mut Campaign,cost:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(cost){return false;}
    if out.column_observer_calls+cost>16384{out.unsupported.insert("column observers capped at 16384 call/world reservations; remaining value worlds not covered".into());return false;}
    out.column_observer_calls+=cost;true
}
fn discharged(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
    inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true))
        &&!bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)
        &&!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)))
        &&!bound.programs.iter().any(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(t,_)|t!=&inv.table)&&super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&inv.table))
}
fn alternative(kind:&C,old:&Value)->Option<Value>{Some(match (kind.required(),old){
    (C::Text{max},Value::String(s)) if s.is_empty()=>if *max>0{Value::String("x".into())}else{return None},
    (C::Text{..},Value::String(s))=>Value::String(s.chars().map(|c|if c=='y'{'x'}else{'y'}).collect()),
    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|s|Value::String((*s).clone())!=*old)?.clone()),
    (C::Principal,Value::Id(s))=>Value::Id(if s==VIEWER{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),
    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(n.checked_add(1)?),
    (C::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>Value::Int(if n<max{n+1}else if n>min{n-1}else{return None}),
    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
    _=>return None,
})}
fn sample_rows(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,inv:&Invariant,row:&Value,presence:bool)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>{
    let column=inv.column.as_ref()?;let kind=bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(column)?;
    let old=match row.field(column){
        Ok(value)=>value.clone(),
        Err(_) if kind.is_optional()&&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].defaults.get(column).is_none_or(|d|matches!(d,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(Value::Null)))=>Value::Null,
        Err(_)=>return None,
    };
    let alt=if presence&&kind.is_optional(){if old==Value::Null{sample(kind.required(),0)?}else{Value::Null}}else{alternative(kind,&old)?};
    let mut out=rows.clone();
    let selected=out.get_mut(&inv.table)?.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())?;
    if let Value::Row(fields)=selected{fields.insert(column.clone(),alt);}else{return None;}
    Some(out)
}
fn same_base_world(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,primary:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,alternate:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,key:&Value)->bool{
    bound.schema.tables.iter().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).all(|(table,definition)|{
        let empty=Vec::new();let rows=primary.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);let other=alternate.get(table).unwrap_or(&empty);
        rows.len()==other.len()&&rows.iter().all(|old|other.iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|{
            let (Value::Row(a),Value::Row(b))=(old,new)else{return false;};
            a.keys().chain(b.keys()).filter(|c|!definition.maintained.contains_key(*c)&&!(table==&inv.table&&inv.column.as_ref()==Some(*c)&&old.field("id").ok()==Some(key))).all(|c|old.field(c).ok()==new.field(c).ok())
        }))
    })
}
// Counterfactual field mutation must not break an authored creator's copies
// or recipient guards. Direct typed payload observations run independently.
fn coupled(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->bool{
    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    if table.rules.insert.needs_field_value(column,&table.columns[column])||table.update_rule_judged().needs_field_value(column,&table.columns[column]){return true;}
    fn definitions(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>){
        if let Some(object)=value.as_object(){
            for tag in ["Const","Let","Assign"]{if let Some(binding)=object.get(tag)&&let (Some(name),Some(value))=(binding.get("name").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binding.get("value")){
                if tag=="Assign"||out.contains_key(name){out.insert(name.into(),serde_json::Value::Null);}else{out.insert(name.into(),value.clone());}
            }}
            for child in object.values(){definitions(child,out);}
        }else if let Some(xs)=value.as_array(){for child in xs{definitions(child,out);}}
    }
    fn fields<'a>(expression:&'a serde_json::Value,bindings:&'a BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,depth:usize)->Option<&'a serde_json::Map<String,serde_json::Value>>{
        if depth>32{return None;}
        if let Some(map)=expression.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return Some(map);}
        if let Some(projection)=expression.get("Builtin")&&projection.get("builtin").is_some_and(|b|b.get("ProjectFields").is_some()){
            return fields(projection.get("args")?.as_array()?.first()?,bindings,depth+1);
        }
        if let Some(name)=expression.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str){return fields(bindings.get(name)?,bindings,depth+1);}
        None
    }
    fn dependency(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,depth:usize)->bool{
        if depth>32{return true;}
        if let Some(field)=value.get("Field")&&let (Some(base),Some(name))=(field.get("base"),field.get("name").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str))&&let Some(map)=fields(base,bindings,depth+1){
            return map.get(name).is_none_or(|expression|dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1));
        }
        if let Some(name)=value.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str){
            if name==arg{return true;}
            if let Some(bound)=bindings.get(name){return bound.is_null()||dependency(bound,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1);}
            return !args.contains_key(name);
        }
        // A guard re-reading the written table may inspect the supplied field
        // through a row alias even when the argument itself occurs only once.
        for tag in ["Get","Exists"]{if value.get(tag).and_then(|v|v.get("table")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(table){return true;}}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|dependency(v,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|dependency(v,arg,table,args,bindings,depth+1)),_=>false}
    }
    fn guarded(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>)->bool{
        for tag in ["Require","Check","If"]{if let Some(condition)=value.get(tag).and_then(|v|v.get("condition"))&&dependency(condition,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}}
        for tag in ["Binary","Unary","Builtin","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"]{if let Some(expression)=value.get(tag){
            if tag=="Builtin"&&expression.get("builtin").is_some_and(|b|b.get("ProjectFields").is_some()){continue;}
            if dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|guarded(v,arg,table,args,bindings)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|guarded(v,arg,table,args,bindings)),_=>false}
    }
    fn related_write(value:&serde_json::Value,arg:&str,table:&str,column:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>)->bool{
        for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
            let Some(write)=value.get(tag)else{continue;};
            let name=write.get("table").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
            if let Some(row)=write.get("row").or_else(||write.get("patch")){
                if let Some(map)=fields(row,bindings,0){
                    if map.iter().any(|(field,expression)|(name!=Some(table)||field!=column)&&dependency(expression,arg,table,args,bindings,0)){return true;}
                }else if dependency(row,arg,table,args,bindings,0){return true;}
            }
            if write.get("key").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|target|target.get("key"))).is_some_and(|key|dependency(key,arg,table,args,bindings,0))||write.get("condition").is_some_and(|condition|dependency(condition,arg,table,args,bindings,0)){return true;}
        }
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>xs.values().any(|v|related_write(v,arg,table,column,args,bindings)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|related_write(v,arg,table,column,args,bindings)),_=>false}
    }
    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,table:&str,column:&str,args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,program:&str,bindings:&BTreeMap<String,serde_json::Value>,guards:&BTreeSet<String>,found:&mut bool,coupled:&mut bool){
        if let Some(object)=value.as_object(){
            for tag in ["Insert","InsertIfAbsent","Upsert","Update","WriteTarget"]{
                let Some(write)=object.get(tag)else{continue;};
                let name=write.get("table").or_else(||write.get("target").and_then(|t|t.get("table"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str);
                if name!=Some(table){continue;}
                let map=write.get("row").or_else(||write.get("patch")).and_then(|e|fields(e,bindings,0));
                let Some(map)=map else{*found=true;*coupled=true;continue;};
                if let Some(expression)=map.get(column){
                    *found=true;
                    if let Some(name)=expression.get("Variable").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)&&args.contains_key(name){
                        let occurrence=serde_json::to_string(&Expr::Variable(name.into())).unwrap();
                        if program.matches(&occurrence).count()!=1||guards.contains(name){*coupled=true;}
                    }else{*coupled=true;}
                }else if matches!(tag,"Insert"|"InsertIfAbsent"|"Upsert")||tag=="WriteTarget"&&write.get("upsert").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_bool)==Some(true){*found=true;*coupled=true;}
            }
            for child in object.values(){writes(child,table,column,args,program,bindings,guards,found,coupled);}
        }else if let Some(xs)=value.as_array(){for child in xs{writes(child,table,column,args,program,bindings,guards,found,coupled);}}
    }
    let(mut found,mut unsafe_writer)=(false,false);
    for p in &bound.programs{
        let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();let text=serde_json::to_string(&p.program).unwrap();let mut bindings=BTreeMap::new();definitions(&json,&mut bindings);
        let guards=p.program.args.keys().filter(|arg|guarded(&json,arg,&inv.table,&p.program.args,&bindings)||related_write(&json,arg,&inv.table,column,&p.program.args,&bindings)).cloned().collect();
        let mut source_found=false;writes(&json,&inv.table,column,&p.program.args,&text,&bindings,&guards,&mut source_found,&mut unsafe_writer);
        // A new compiler insertion form cannot silently become an independent
        // source merely because this reduction did not recognize its payload.
        if !source_found&&ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(t,k)|t==&inv.table&&matches!(*k,"insert"|"upsert")){source_found=true;unsafe_writer=true;}
        found|=source_found;
    }
    found&&unsafe_writer
}
fn run_pair_variant(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,presence:bool,out:&mut Campaign){
    if discharged(bound,inv){return;}
    if coupled(bound,inv){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: alternate value may break authored creator/guard coupling; only actual typed payloads and committed histories count",inv.target_label()));return;}
    if !reserve(out,1){return;}
    let column=inv.column.as_deref().unwrap();
    let kind=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns[column];
    let mut other=match sample_rows(bound,rows,inv,row,presence){
        Some(other)=>other,
        None if kind.contains_asset()&&fixture.is_some()=>{
            let mut other=rows.clone();
            let Some(value)=sample(kind.required(),4)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media type unsupported",inv.table,column));return;};
            if let Some(Value::Row(fields))=other.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())){fields.insert(column.into(),value);}other
        },
        None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column pair needs a second typed value and, for media, a coupled verified fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;},
    };
    // Both worlds receive the same verified ledger, including the alternate
    // upload. Only the protected row value changes; no fabricated media ids.
    let ledger;
    let fixture=if kind.contains_asset(){
        let Some(base)=fixture else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: media column pair requires the fixture ledger",inv.table,column));return;};
        let mut prepared=match fork(bound,base,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
        let target=other.get_mut(&inv.table).unwrap().iter_mut().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
        if let Value::Row(fields)=target {
            let value=match hydrate(&mut prepared,kind,&fields[column]){Ok(v)=>v,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: alternate media upload: {why}",inv.table,column));return;}};
            fields.insert(column.into(),value);
        }
        ledger=prepared;Some(&ledger)
    }else{fixture};
    let altrow=other[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).unwrap();
    let observing=histories::observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
    let mut actors=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for r in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=r.field(c)&&!actors.contains(p){actors.push(p.clone());}}}}
    'actors: for viewer in &actors{
        if fixture.is_some()&&out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&histories::trace_key(&v.history)==histories::trace_key(trace.map(|(_,s)|s).unwrap_or(&[]))){continue;}

        {
            if !reserve(out,2){return;}
            let judge=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,rows,program("$column.intent",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))};
            let Ok(mut judge)=judge else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: primary column intent world unavailable",inv.target_label()));continue;};
            let actual=match readback(&judge){Ok(rows)=>rows,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);continue;}};
            let Some(primary)=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok())else{continue;};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,primary,&mut judge)!=Ok(false){continue;}
        }
        for bp in &bound.programs{
            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
            let p=&bp.program;
            if ephemeral_operation(bound,p,out){continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap();
            if !observing.iter().any(|table|super::super::audit::mentions(&json,table)){continue;}
            if !matches!(p.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.native{continue;}
            let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
            if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|s|json.contains(s)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque column observer declined",p.name));continue;}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation {
                if inv.policy.is_none(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation has unknown policy dependencies; disclosure-boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(|r|stability::dependencies(bound,r)).unwrap_or_default();
                if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){
                    out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column mutation can change protected row/policy before disclosure; boundary tracing required",p.name));continue;
                }
                if stability::implicit(bound,p).iter().any(|t|t==&inv.table||deps.contains(t)){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: implicit writes can change column policy before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
            }
            let args_list=histories::fixture_cases(p,rows,&actors,out);
            for args in args_list{
                if !reserve(out,12){return;}
                let execute=|alternate:bool|->Result<(serde_json::Value,BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,BTreeMap<String,Value>),String>{
                    let seeds=if alternate{&other}else{rows};
                    let mut e=if let Some(fixture)=fixture{
                        let mut e=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())?;
                        if alternate{
                            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            let Value::Row(fields)=altrow else{return Err("not a row".into());};
                            let clean=Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect());
                            if let Err(why)=e.seed_at([(inv.table.clone(),clean.clone())],NOW){
                                let immutable=t.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|fields.contains(c)))||matches!(c,Constraint::AppendOnly))||matches!(t.columns[column].required(),C::Image{once:true,..});
                                if !immutable{return Err(why.to_string());}
                                if fixture.ephemeral_hub().snapshot().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.version.epoch!=0{return Err("immutable column alternate would drop existing ephemeral state; reduction uncovered".into());}
                                e=engine_on_store(bound,&other,Vec::new(),fixture.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
                            }
                        }e
                    }else{engine(bound,seeds,program("$column.pair",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))?};
                    validate_fixture(bound,&mut e)?;
                    let actual=readback(&e)?;
                    let current=actual.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()).ok_or("column target is absent after validation")?;
                    if policy_in(bound,inv,&actual,viewer,None,current,&mut e)!=Ok(false){return Err("validated column-world policy is not false".into());}
                    let actual_args=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,&args,viewer)?;
                    let observed=paired::observe(&mut e,p,viewer,actual_args.clone())?;
                    Ok((observed,actual,actual_args))
                };
                let (a,primary_rows,primary_args,b,alternate_rows,alternate_args)=match (execute(false),execute(true)){
                    (Ok((a,primary,aa)),Ok((b,alternate,ba)))=>(a,primary,aa,b,alternate,ba),
                    (Err(why),_)|(_,Err(why))=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{} / {}: declined column world: {why}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),p.name));continue;}
                };
                out.attempted+=2;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=2;
                if primary_args!=alternate_args{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired column arguments require a coupled asset ledger; differing materialized inputs declined",p.name));continue;}
                let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
                if !same_base_world(bound,inv,&primary_rows,&alternate_rows,key){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: committed worlds change non-protected base fields/captures or populations; reduction unavailable",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));continue;}
                if a==b{continue;}
                if !matches!((execute(false),execute(true)),(Ok((aa,pa,aa_args)),Ok((bb,pb,bb_args))) if aa==a&&bb==b&&pa==primary_rows&&pb==alternate_rows&&aa_args==primary_args&&bb_args==alternate_args){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: repeated column inputs/worlds/output are unstable or unavailable",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                let actual_row=primary_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
                let actual_alternate=alternate_rows[&inv.table].iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(key)).unwrap();
                out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:if fixture.is_some(){Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace}else{Reachability::SyntheticOperation},invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:primary_args,viewer:viewer.clone(),operation:p.name.clone(),effect:"column read observation".into(),row:actual_row.clone(),old:Some(actual_alternate.clone()),supporting_rows:trace.map(|(initial,_)|initial.clone()).unwrap_or_else(||primary_rows.clone()),history:trace.map(|(_,steps)|steps.to_vec()).unwrap_or_default(),rule_path:format!("authored observer depends on {}.{} while policy is false in both typed worlds",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()),scope:if fixture.is_some(){"primary observation executes on trusted fixture; alternate column is a dependency test, not an authored alternate-history claim"}else{"schema-valid synthetic worlds; authored observer, no application reachability claim"}.into(),observations:Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:alternate_rows})});
                continue 'actors;
            }
        }
    }
}
fn run_pair(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,row:&Value,fixture:Option<&Engine>,trace:Option<(&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,&[histories::Step])>,out:&mut Campaign){
    run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,false,out);
    if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(c)).is_some_and(C::is_optional){run_pair_variant(bound,inv,rows,row,fixture,trace,true,out);}
}
pub(super) fn generated(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")&&sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))){
        if inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}.{}: column clock pairs use the campaign clock only; other temporal ticks not covered",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap()));}
        for variant in 0..16{
            if !reserve(out,1){return;}
            let rows=match snapshot(bound,inv,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column snapshot: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let e=match engine(bound,&rows,program("$column.seed",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: column seed: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
            for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,None,None,out);}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()){break;}
        }
    }
}
pub(super) fn fixture(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(rows)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),None,out);}
    }
}

pub(super) fn after_history(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,steps:&[histories::Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    let rows=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id")){

        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){if out.exhausted(){return;}run_pair(bound,inv,&rows,row,Some(fixture),Some((initial,steps)),out);}
    }
}

+fn relationship_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{

 fn run(&self,args:&[&str])->Output{Command::new(BIN).args(args).current_dir(&self.0).env_remove("PORT_LO").env_remove("PORT_HI").output().unwrap()}
}
impl Drop for App{fn drop(&mut self){let _=fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
fn text(o:&Output)->String{format!("{}\n{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout),String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stderr))}

//! Role authority is compared by effective gates, never by names like 'admin'.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — advisory grant-edge analysis, not a security proof or implicit exemption.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn probe_pairs(bound:&Bound,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[RuleTerm],equalities:&[(String,RuleTerm)])->Vec<(String,RuleTerm)>{
    let mut pairs=equalities.to_vec();
    if let Some(idx)=bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.index(index)){for (component,term) in idx.components.iter().zip(key){if let Some(column)=component.column(){pairs.push((column.into(),term.clone()));}}}
    pairs
}
pub(super) fn global_principal(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    let t=bound.schema.table(table)?;
    t.indexes.values().find_map(|index|{
        if !index.unique||index.components.len()!=1{return None;}
        let column=index.components[0].column()?;
        matches!(t.columns.get(column),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).then(||column.to_string())
    })
}
pub(super) fn membership_probe(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{
    let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=rule else{return false;};
    if table!=roster{return false;}
    let Some(principal)=global_principal(bound,table)else{return false;};
    let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
    pairs.len()==1&&pairs[0].0==principal&&pairs[0].1==RuleTerm::Viewer
}
// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — ownership is required for every supplied owned field;
// only an exclusively owned patch bypasses an unnamed row update gate.
fn write_admission(table:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->RuleExpr{
    let touched=fields.iter().filter(|f|!f.spread&&f.name!="id").map(|f|f.name.as_str()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let owned_only=!touched.is_empty()&&!fields.iter().any(|f|f.spread)&&touched.iter().all(|f|table.written_by.contains_key(*f));
    let gate=match op{
        ast::WriteOp::Insert=>table.rules.insert.clone(),
        ast::WriteOp::Update if owned_only&&!table.update_transition=>RuleExpr::Allow,
        ast::WriteOp::Update=>table.update_rule_judged().into_owned(),
        _=>RuleExpr::Deny,
    };
    let mut owners=table.written_by.iter().filter(|(field,authority)|touched.contains(field.as_str())||op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&!authority.audience&&table.rules.update.needs_field_value(field,&table.columns[*field])).map(|(_,authority)|RuleExpr::Eq(if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{RuleTerm::Field(authority.owner.clone())}else{RuleTerm::OldField(authority.owner.clone())},RuleTerm::Viewer)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    owners.push(RuleExpr::Or(gate.disjuncts().iter().filter(|r|r.service_grant().is_none()).map(|r|(*r).clone()).collect()));
    RuleExpr::And(owners)
}
pub(super) fn membership_grant_risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,operation:&str,requirements:&[RuleExpr],op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->bool{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Insert,fields)||membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Update,fields);}
    fn honors(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster)),
        r=>membership_probe(bound,r,roster),
    }}
    if global_principal(bound,roster).is_none()||!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster))){return false;}
    let table=&bound.schema.tables[roster];
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&global_principal(bound,roster).is_some_and(|column|kernel::implies(&admission,&RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.clone()),RuleTerm::Field(column)))==Ok(true)){return false;}
    let ordinary=substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster","$membership",None,None);
    bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.is_none()&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).any(|p|super::operation::safe(p)&&kernel::implies(&ordinary,p)==Ok(false))
}
fn substitute(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,role:Option<&str>,target:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    let known=|t:&RuleTerm|match t{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c) if c==column=>target.map(|s|snapback4_core::Value::String(s.into())),RuleTerm::Literal(v)=>Some(v.clone()),_=>None};
    match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,index,equalities,..} if table==roster&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
            let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
            let requested=pairs.iter().find(|(c,_)|c==column).and_then(|(_,t)|if let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{Some(s.as_str())}else{None});
            if requested.is_none(){rule.clone()}else if requested==role{
                if pairs.iter().all(|(c,t)|c==column||matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{rule.clone()}
            }else{RuleExpr::Deny}
        },
        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)),RuleTerm::Viewer) if seat.starts_with("service:")=>RuleExpr::Deny,
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(known(a),known(b)){if (a==b)==matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Eq(..)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}}else{rule.clone()},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Not(r)=>RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target))),
        _=>rule.clone(),
    }
}
pub(super) fn risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str,operation:&str,targets:&[String],op:ast::WriteOp,requirements:&[RuleExpr],dynamic:bool,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->Option<String>{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Insert,requirements,dynamic,fields).or_else(||risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Update,requirements,dynamic,fields));}
    let table=bound.schema.table(roster)?;
    if requirements.iter().any(|r|!r.disjuncts().is_empty()&&r.disjuncts().iter().all(|b|b.service_grant().is_some())){return None;}
    let roles=role_values(bound,roster,column);
    let intents=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c==column)&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    fn role_guards(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut Vec<RuleExpr>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|role_columns(bound,table).contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))))=>out.push(rule.clone()),
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{role_guards(bound,r,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&kernel::implies(&admission,&RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.into()),RuleTerm::Field(column.into())))==Ok(true){return None;}
    let mut guards=Vec::new();role_guards(bound,&admission,&mut guards);
    let independent=kernel::implies(&substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster",column,None,None),&RuleExpr::Or(guards))==Ok(true);
    let mut ranked=roles.iter().filter(|r|targets.is_empty()||targets.contains(r)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let score=|role:&&String|bound.schema.visible_tables().flat_map(|(_,t)|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]).filter(|rule|kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,Some(role.as_str()),None))==Ok(true)).count();
    ranked.sort_by_key(|role|(std::cmp::Reverse(score(role)),role.as_str()));
    for target in ranked{
        for caller in std::iter::once(None).chain(roles.iter().map(|s|Some(s.as_str()))){
            if caller==Some(target.as_str()){continue;}
            let admitted=substitute(bound,&admission,roster,column,caller,Some(target));
            let admits=kernel::implies(&admitted,&RuleExpr::Deny)!=Ok(true);
            if !admits{continue;}
            let violates_intent=intents.iter().any(|p|kernel::implies(&admitted,&substitute(bound,p,roster,column,caller,Some(target)))==Ok(false));
            // Existing independently gated appointers may delegate different
            // roles. Independent intent can restrict a particular target role.
            if independent&&!violates_intent{continue;}
            if violates_intent{return Some(format!("{roster}.{column} assignment to '{target}' is not established by the independent delegation intent for {}",caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)]{
                let granted=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,Some(target),None);let previous=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,caller,None);
                if kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&granted)!=Ok(true){continue;}
                if !dynamic&&!(matches!(effect,"read"|"delete")||!role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()){continue;}
                if kernel::implies(&granted,&previous)==Ok(false){return Some(format!("{} can confer '{target}', which adds {name}.{effect} authority not held by {}",roster,caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            }}
        }
    }None
}

pub(super) fn role_columns(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut columns=BTreeSet::new();
    if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table){for c in ["role","kind"]{if t.columns.contains_key(c){columns.insert(c.into());}}}
    fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,table:&str,columns:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table:t,index,key,equalities,..} if t==table&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>for (c,v) in &probe_pairs(bound,t,index,key,equalities){if matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))){columns.insert(c.clone());}},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,table,columns)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,table,columns),_=>{}
    }}
    let named=columns.clone();let mut observed=BTreeSet::new();
    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,table,&mut observed);}}
    let global=bound.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|t|t.indexes.values().any(|index|{
        if !index.unique{return false;}
        let Some(parts)=index.components.iter().map(|p|p.column()).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()else{return false;};
        let principals=parts.iter().filter(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(**c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))).count();
        if principals!=1{return false;}
        parts.iter().all(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(*c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))||named.contains(*c))
            ||named.is_empty()&&observed.len()==1&&parts.len()==2&&parts.iter().all(|c|observed.contains(*c)||matches!(t.columns.get(*c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)))
    }));
    if global{for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,table,&mut columns);}}}else{columns.clear();}
    columns
}

pub(super) fn role_values(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str)->Vec<String>{
    let Some(table)=bound.schema.table(roster)else{return Vec::new();};
    let Some(kind)=table.columns.get(column)else{return Vec::new();};
    match kind.required(){snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Enum{variants}=>variants.clone(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Text{..}=>{
        let mut roles=BTreeSet::new();
        fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,roles:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
            RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if table==roster=>for (c,t) in &probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities){if c==column&&let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{roles.insert(s.clone());}},
            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles),_=>{}
        }}
        for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,roster,column,&mut roles);}}roles.into_iter().collect()
    },_=>Vec::new()}
}

//! `check`'s advisory notes, ranked. A green check printed nine notes in
//! source order, and builders could not tell an FYI about offline coverage
//! from a page that never completes. Notes stay advisory (they never fail a
//! check); this only orders them, names each group, and shortens repeats.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum Tier {
    /// Can cost correctness or completeness: a page that never completes, a
    /// write no caller can pass, a disclosure, a read a device cannot answer.
    Review,
    /// Costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage.
    Performance,
    /// What a declaration implies; nothing to fix for online use.
    Design,
}

pub(crate) fn tier(code: &str) -> Tier {
    match code {
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" | "N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" | "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" | "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" | "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" | "N_PATCH_CLEARS" | "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_VACUOUS_RULE" | "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" | "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" | "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_SHAPE_METER" | "N_HELD_UNKNOWN" | "N_SEQUENCE_READ"
        | "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" | "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" | "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"
        | "N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED" | "N_SEARCH_OPEN" | "N_TIMELINE_PROFILE_CAP" | "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => Tier::Review,
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.4 — a cursorless `first N` is what the declaration says: the first N rows (wave 3 #7).
        "N_SYNC_ORDER" | "N_NO_HORIZON" | "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED" | "N_TIMELINE_COMPAT" | "N_NO_CURSOR" => Tier::Design,
        _ => Tier::Performance,
    }
}

/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){
    (tier(code),match code{
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"=>0,"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"=>1,
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"=>3,"N_BROAD_READ"=>4,"N_SECURITY_INTENT"=>5,_=>2,
    })
}

/// The first words of a note's line: what to do about it, or that nothing
/// needs doing (wave 7 #11: "put a one-line verdict on every advisory note").
// @ref LLP 3000.036#17.7 — the verdict leads; the explanation follows it.
pub(crate) fn verdict(code: &str, summary: &str, form: bool) -> &'static str {
    match code {
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" if summary.starts_with("persona-only:") => "no action without use identity",
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" => "fix: add a production bootstrap writer",
        "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" => "fix: update the fixture arguments",
        "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" => "review: complete for the pinned parent audience; other callers may be capped",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("the complete form is to add `cap ") => "fix: add the exact source cap below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("no verified complete form for this read") => "review: no verified complete form for this read; expect `capped` and show what arrived",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form && summary.contains("the closest form is") => "fix: use the closest form below (one arm stays `complete: false`), or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form => "fix: use the complete form below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" => "fix: pin the scope, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" => "fix: declare the reference's `on delete`, or guard the delete",
        "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" => "review: some deleters get NOT_FOUND",
        "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" => "review: this reference argument has no mandatory target lookup",
        "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" => "review: hidden and absent are different caller outcomes",
        "N_PATCH_CLEARS" => "review: an omitted argument clears this column",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" if summary.contains("admits only 'service:") => "review: a seat require grants no table authority",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" => "fix: this write refuses E_RULE for callers you admit",
        "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" => "review: callers you admit may still get E_RULE",
        "N_QUERY_FILTERED" => "review: this query branch may filter rows under table authority",
        "N_READ_DENIED" => "fix: this read always returns null",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" if summary.contains("get NOT_FOUND") => "review: the hidden lookup runs before this require",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" => "fix: this require cannot see the rows it tests",
        "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" => "fix: this nullable lookup is always null for the admitted callers",
        "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" => "review: a required read gate may hide rows from writers",
        "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" => "review: who can read this",
        "N_VACUOUS_RULE" => "review: who can write this",
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" => "review: readable conflicts require the original immutable value",
        "N_NO_CURSOR" => "no action for a top-N list",
        _ => match tier(code) {
            Tier::Review => "review",
            Tier::Performance => "no action unless it is slow (`check --cost` measures it)",
            Tier::Design => "no action for an online app",
        },
    }
}

fn heading(tier: Tier) -> &'static str {
    match tier {
        Tier::Review => "review — can cost correctness or completeness:",
        Tier::Performance => "performance — costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage:",
        Tier::Design => "by design — what a declaration implies; no action needed for online use:",
    }
}

pub(crate) struct Note {
    pub site: String,
    pub code: String,
    pub summary: String,
    /// An expanded compiled repair: its lead line and the code. Compact
    /// completeness notes leave this empty and keep the repair in `--verbose`.
    pub form: Option<(String, String)>,
    /// Three-line default rendering for completeness verdicts. Their full
    /// planner proof and compiled repair remain in `check --verbose`.
    pub compact: Option<[String; 3]>,
}

/// The compiled form leading a note's rewrite: the text before "Otherwise:",
/// when it holds code (a lead line ending in `:` and the declaration under it).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.5 — the compiled owner-or-X rewrite prints by default, once per form.
pub(crate) fn compiled_form(code: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    if !matches!(code, "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE") { return None; }
    let head = rewrite.split("\n\nOtherwise:").next()?;
    let (lead, body) = head.split_once('\n')?;
    (lead.ends_with(':') && body.trim_start().starts_with("query ")).then(|| (lead.to_owned(), body.trim_end().to_owned()))
}

/// The ranked block printed after `check: ok`; empty for no notes.
pub(crate) fn render(notes: Vec<Note>) -> Vec<String> {
    if notes.is_empty() { return Vec::new(); }
    // Collapse one code's repeats at one site, keeping first-seen order.
    let mut unique: Vec<(Note, usize)> = Vec::new();
    for note in notes {
        match unique.iter_mut().find(|(seen, _)| seen.site == note.site && seen.code == note.code && seen.summary == note.summary && seen.form == note.form && seen.compact == note.compact) {
            Some((_, count)) => *count += 1,
            None => unique.push((note, 1)),
        }
    }
    let total = unique.len();
    // Rank by tier; within a tier, a code's notes stay together in the order the code first appeared.
    let first_seen = |code: &str, unique: &[(Note, usize)]| unique.iter().position(|(n, _)| n.code == code).unwrap();
    let mut order: Vec<usize> = (0..unique.len()).collect();
    order.sort_by_key(|&i| (tier(&unique[i].0.code), first_seen(&unique[i].0.code, &unique), i));
    let mut lines = vec![format!("{total} advisory note{} — notes never block dev, test or deploy (most consequential first; check --verbose explains each, snapback4 why <CODE> one):",
        if total == 1 { "" } else { "s" })];
    let mut current = None;
    let mut shown_codes = Vec::new();

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Limits {
    pub principal_rows: u64,
    pub principal_bytes: u64,
    pub rows: u64,
    pub bytes: u64,
}
impl Default for Limits {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            principal_rows: 1000,
            principal_bytes: 4 * 1024 * 1024,
            rows: 100_000,
            bytes: 512 * 1024 * 1024,
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
struct Count {
    rows: u64,
    bytes: u64,
}
impl Count {
    fn add(&mut self, bytes: u64) {
        self.rows += 1;
        self.bytes = self.bytes.saturating_add(bytes);
    }
    fn remove(&mut self, bytes: u64) {
        self.rows -= 1;
        self.bytes -= bytes;
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
struct Accounting {
    owners: BTreeMap<(String, String), (String, u64)>,
    principals: BTreeMap<String, Count>,
    total: Count,
}
struct Plan {
    changes: Vec<((String, String), Option<(String, u64)>)>,
    principals: BTreeMap<String, Count>,
    total: Count,
}
impl Accounting {
    fn remove(&mut self, key: &(String, String)) {
        if let Some((principal, bytes)) = self.owners.remove(key) {
            self.total.remove(bytes);
            let count = self.principals.get_mut(&principal).unwrap();
            count.remove(bytes);
            if count.rows == 0 {
                self.principals.remove(&principal);
            }
        }
    }
    fn plan(
        &self,
        layer: &Layered<'_>,
        schema: &Schema,
        viewer: &str,
        limits: Limits,
    ) -> Result<Plan, RuntimeError> {
        let mut plan = Plan {
            changes: Vec::new(),
            principals: BTreeMap::new(),
            total: self.total,
        };
        for key in layer.changed() {
            if let Some((owner, bytes)) = self.owners.get(&key) {
                plan.total.remove(*bytes);
                plan.principals
                    .entry(owner.clone())
                    .or_insert(self.principals[owner])
                    .remove(*bytes);
            }
            let owner = if layer.eph.get(&key.0, &key.1)?.is_some() {
                let size = declared_size(&schema.tables[&key.0]);
                plan.total.add(size);
                plan.principals
                    .entry(viewer.into())
                    .or_insert_with(|| self.principals.get(viewer).copied().unwrap_or_default())
                    .add(size);
                Some((viewer.into(), size))
            } else {
                None
            };
            plan.changes.push((key, owner));
        }
        if plan.total.rows > limits.rows
            || plan.total.bytes > limits.bytes
            || plan
                .principals
                .values()
                .any(|c| c.rows > limits.principal_rows || c.bytes > limits.principal_bytes)
        {
            return Err(full());
        }
        Ok(plan)
    }
    fn apply(&mut self, plan: Plan) {
        identity: &str,
        generation: u64,
        schema: &Schema,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if identity != self.identity || generation < self.generation {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
                "ephemeral hub serves a newer store or generation; retry with a fresh reader"
                    .into(),
            ));
        }
        if generation > self.generation {
            self.rebind(identity.into(), generation, schema);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn sweep(&mut self, now: i64) -> Result<usize, RuntimeError> {
        let mut removed = 0;
        for (name, table) in &self.tables {
            loop {
                let rows = self.rows.scan(
                    name,
                    "$expiry",
                    &[],
                    &Bounds {
                        lte: Some(Value::Int(now)),
                        ..Bounds::default()
                    },
                    Dir::Asc,
                    256,
                )?;
                if rows.is_empty() {
                    break;
                }
                for (row, _) in rows {
                    let id = row.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
                    self.rows.delete(name, table, &row)?;
                    self.accounting.remove(&(name.clone(), id));
                    removed += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(removed)
    }
}
impl Hub {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — campaign copies preserve deadlines, quotas and epochs without retaining the live publisher.
    pub(super) fn fork_fixture(&self,identity:&str,generation:u64)->Result<Self,RuntimeError>{
        let state=self.lock()?;
        if state.identity!=identity||state.generation!=generation{return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs a hub bound to the selected store generation".into()));}
        Ok(Self{limits:self.limits,state:Mutex::new(HubState{rows:state.rows.fork_fixture(),identity:state.identity.clone(),generation:state.generation,tables:state.tables.clone(),accounting:state.accounting.clone(),version:state.version.clone(),ring:state.ring.clone(),preserved:state.preserved.clone(),publisher:None})})
    }
    pub fn new(identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) -> Self {
        Self::with_limits(identity, generation, schema, Limits::default())
    }
    pub fn with_limits(identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema, limits: Limits) -> Self {
        Self {
            state: Mutex::new(HubState::new(identity, generation, schema)),
            limits,
        }
    }
    pub fn lock(&self) -> Result<MutexGuard<'_, HubState>, RuntimeError> {
        self.state
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| RuntimeError::Store("ephemeral hub lock poisoned".into()))
    }
    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> Result<Snapshot, RuntimeError> {
        Ok(self.lock()?.snapshot())
    }
    /// Install before serving. Called under the hub lock after each commit or
    /// rebind; the callback may take the wake mutex, never the hub or engine.
    // @ref LLP 3000.017#3.3 — publish before releasing the hub.
    pub fn set_publisher(&self, publisher: impl Fn(Snapshot) + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let mut hub = self.lock()?;
        hub.publisher = Some(Arc::new(publisher));
        hub.publish();
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn clear_and_rebind(
        &self,
        identity: String,
        generation: u64,
        schema: &Schema,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.lock()?.rebind(identity, generation, schema);
        Ok(())
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — never hold the hub across SQLite activation.
    // Readers take SQLite before the hub. Even BEGIN IMMEDIATE only reserves
    // the DELETE-mode writer: commit/spill still needs readers to release it.
    // Adoption checks poison before activation, then releases the hub. A panic
    // racing that check cannot turn a committed adoption into a refusal: rebind
    // replaces all ephemeral state, so discard poisoned state and clear poison.
    pub(super) fn rebind_committed(&self, identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) {
        let mut hub = self.state.lock().unwrap_or_else(std::sync::PoisonError::into_inner);
        hub.rebind(identity, generation, schema);
        self.state.clear_poison();
    }
    pub fn sweep(&self, now: i64) -> Result<usize, RuntimeError> {
        self.lock()?.sweep(now)
    }
}

pub struct Outcome {
    pub result: Value,
    pub ephemeral: Change,
    pub metered: Metered,
    pub seq: u64,
}
impl Engine {
    pub fn ephemeral_hub(&self) -> Arc<Hub> {
        self.ephemeral.clone()
    }
    /// Engine replacement calls this under its owner mutex, before it is published.
    pub fn share_ephemeral_hub(&mut self, hub: Arc<Hub>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        hub.clear_and_rebind(
            self.store.meta("store_id")?.unwrap_or_default(),
            self.generation,
            &self.schema,
        )?;
        self.ephemeral = hub;
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn mutate_ephemeral(
        &mut self,
        name: &str,
        mut context: Context,
    ) -> Result<Outcome, RuntimeError> {
        let program = self
            .programs
            .get(name)
            .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::UnknownOp(name.into()))?;
        if program.kind != ProgramKind::Mutation
            || !self
                .schema
                .operations
                .get(name)

//! Saved-source fixture selection, business cases and the query sweep.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#9-wave-13-saved-version-proofs-day3-2026-10-16 — contemporary fixtures belong to the selected saved source.
use super::*;

const CASE_SUMMARY: &str = "SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES";

fn case_count(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
    Ok(text.lines().filter(|line| {
        let line = line.trim();
        !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with("//")
    }).count())
}

pub fn test(options: &Options) -> i32 {

            if path.is_dir() { path.join("fixtures") }

            else { path.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures", path.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())) }

//! The same fixture loader serves query testing and residency at either CLI entry point.
use super::Options;
use crate::project::Project;
use snapback4_core::{Engine, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;

pub(super) fn engine(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    engine_at(project, bound, options, snapback4_core::now_ms())
}

pub(super) fn engine_at(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, options: &Options, now: i64) -> Result<Engine, String> {
    let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture") else {
        if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Engine::new_at(store, bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
        }
        return project.open_engine(bound).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open the store: {error}"));
    };
    let label=options.flags.get("fixture-input-label").unwrap_or(fixture);
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut schema = bound.schema.clone();
    // Residency defers ordinary maintains until after bulk loading and builds
    // them once; column derives stay installed and maintain as rows insert.
    let residency = options.flags.contains_key("residency");
    if residency { schema.maintains.retain(|m| m.private.is_some()); }
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::memory().map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut engine = Engine::new_at(store, schema, bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot open a store: {error}"))?;
    let mut names = Vec::new();
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(fixture).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {label}: {error}"))? {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture directory {label}: {error}"))?;
        names.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
    }
    names.sort();
    // One batch for the whole directory: the engine orders parents before
    // children within a batch, so file order (and read_dir order) cannot make
    // a read-through child or a reference miss its parent.
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    let mut unknown = Vec::new();
    for name in names {
        let Some(table) = name.strip_suffix(".jsonl") else { continue };
        if name == super::test_cases::FILE { continue; }
        let path = std::path::Path::new(fixture).join(&name);
        let shown_path=std::path::Path::new(label).join(&name);
        let Some(definition) = bound.schema.tables.get(table) else {
            if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") {
                eprintln!("test: skipped {} — saved source declares no table {table}; pass --fixture <dir> to select contemporary fixtures explicitly", shown_path.display());
                continue;
            }
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — the starter's own unmodified sample is skipped loudly; a project's file for a missing table still refuses.
            if !options.flags.contains_key("source") && super::init::starter_fixture(&name).is_some_and(|starter| std::fs::read_to_string(&path).is_ok_and(|text| text == starter)) {
                eprintln!("test: WARNING: skipped {} — the todo starter's unmodified sample rows for a `{table}` table your snapback/*.q does not declare; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's other leftovers", shown_path.display());
                continue;
            }
            unknown.push(name);
            continue;
        };
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read fixture file {}: {error}", shown_path.display()))?;
        for (line_number, line) in text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty()) {
            let at = format!("{name}:{}", line_number + 1);
            let mut json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}"))?;
            // A residency fixture is often an exported store: engine-maintained
            // columns are dropped and rebuilt from the source rows. `test`
            // refuses them as an insert and `/seed` do (E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN).
            if residency && let Some(fields) = json.as_object_mut() { fields.retain(|field, _| !definition.maintained.contains_key(field)); }
            relative_times(definition, &mut json, now).map_err(|why| format!("{at}: {why}"))?;
            if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &json) { return Err(format!("{at}: {why}")); }
            let row = wire::row_from_json(definition, &json).map_err(|error| format!("{at}: {error}\n  hint: a fixture row is one JSON object per line with the table's fields and an \"id\"; see snapback4 guide testing"))?;
            rows.push((table.to_owned(), row));
        }
    }
    // A misspelled file name would otherwise load nothing and still pass.
    if !unknown.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<&str> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
        // 0.4.17's init wrote the todo starter's rows beside a project's own schema.
        let starter = unknown.iter().any(|name| name == "todos.jsonl")
            && std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(fixture).join("todos.jsonl")).is_ok_and(|text| text.contains("\"id\":\"todo-milk\""));
        let starter = if starter { format!("\n  hint: todos.jsonl is the todo starter's sample that `snapback4 init` wrote; `snapback4 init --adapt` removes it and the starter's {} while they are unmodified (or delete them, and the starter's list/add/toggle/remove lines, yourself)", super::test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
        return Err(format!("{label}: {} name{} no table in snapback/*.q; nothing was run{starter}\n  hint: a fixture file is <table>.jsonl for a declared table ({}), plus {}; see snapback4 guide testing",
            unknown.join(", "), if unknown.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, tables.join(", "), super::test_cases::FILE));
    }
    // Fixture rows take `/seed`'s insert path (defaults, sequences, unique with the author's codes, caps, maintained views), not the trusted bulk import.
    let loaded = engine.seed_at(rows, now).map_err(|error| {
        let text = error.to_string();
        let hint = if text.contains("NOT_FOUND") { "\n  hint: every fixture file loads as one batch, parents first; NOT_FOUND means a referenced or read-through parent row is in no fixture file (check the id)" } else { "" };
        format!("{label}: {text}{hint}")
    })?;
    if residency {
        engine.adopt_at(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()), now).map_err(|error| format!("cannot build fixture maintains: {error}"))?;
    }
    if !options.json { println!("test: loaded {loaded} rows from {label} in {:.1} s", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64()); }
    Ok(engine)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000#5-fixtures-and-dev-evidence — relative time leaves, seeds and operations share one captured clock.
/// Only typed time leaves interpret `now`: text and JSON retain their literals.
pub(crate) fn relative_times(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, row: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(fields) = row.as_object_mut() {
        for (name, value) in fields {
            if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(name) { time_value(kind, value, now).map_err(|why| format!("{name}: {why}"))?; }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn time_value(kind: &snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType, value: &mut serde_json::Value, now: i64) -> Result<(), String> {
    use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) if !value.is_null() => time_value(inner, value, now),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, serde_json::Value::Array(items)) => {
            for item in items { time_value(of, item, now)?; }
            Ok(())
        }

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, serde::Serialize)]
pub struct Change {
    pub preserved: Vec<String>,
    pub epoch: u64,
    pub incarnation: String,
    pub tables: Vec<String>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub preserved: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    pub identity: String,
    pub generation: u64,
    pub version: Version,
    pub ring: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    pub tables: Vec<String>,
}
impl Snapshot {
    pub fn preserved_since(&self, epoch: u64, incarnation: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        if incarnation != self.version.incarnation || epoch >= self.version.epoch { return vec![]; }
        let mut result: Option<Vec<String>> = None;
        let mut previous = epoch;
        for (at, tables) in self.preserved.iter().filter(|(at,_)| *at > epoch) {
            if *at != previous + 1 { return vec![]; }
            if let Some(result) = &mut result { result.retain(|t| tables.contains(t)); } else { result = Some(tables.clone()); }
            previous = *at;
        }
        if previous != self.version.epoch { return vec![]; }
        result.unwrap_or_default()
    }

    pub fn changed(
        &self,
        epoch: u64,
        incarnation: Option<&str>,
        interest: Option<&BTreeSet<String>>,
    ) -> Vec<String> {
        let allowed = |name: &String| interest.is_none_or(|set| set.contains(name));
        if incarnation.is_some_and(|id| id != self.version.incarnation)
            || epoch > self.version.epoch
            || self
                .ring
                .front()
                .is_some_and(|(first, _)| epoch.saturating_add(1) < *first)
        {
            return match interest {
                Some(tables) => tables.iter().cloned().collect(),
                None => self.tables.clone(),
            };
        }
        self.ring
            .iter()
            .filter(|(at, _)| *at > epoch)
            .flat_map(|(_, tables)| tables.iter())
            .filter(|t| allowed(t))
            .cloned()
            .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>()
            .into_iter()
            .collect()
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Limits {
    pub principal_rows: u64,
    pub principal_bytes: u64,
                }
                None => {
                    self.owners.remove(&key);
                }
            }
        }
        for (owner, count) in plan.principals {
            if count.rows == 0 {
                self.principals.remove(&owner);
            } else {
                self.principals.insert(owner, count);
            }
        }
        self.total = plan.total;
    }
}
/// Prices the declared image plus every bounded index key; no payload is consulted.
pub fn declared_size(table: &Table) -> u64 {
    let image = super::write::target::row_copy_ceiling(table);
    table.indexes.values().fold(image, |total, index| {
        let key = index
            .columns()
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|c| table.columns.get(c))
            .fold(144u64, |n, t| {
                n.saturating_add(
                    16 + super::write::target::column_copy_ceiling(t).saturating_mul(2),
                )
            });
        total.saturating_add(64 + key.min(crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as u64))
    })
}
fn full() -> RuntimeError {
    RuntimeError::Structural {
        code: "E_EPHEMERAL_FULL",
        message: "ephemeral capacity is full; wait for expiry or delete your rows and retry".into(),
    }
}
fn incarnation() -> String {
    crate::ids::mint(crate::now_ms(), &mut crate::ids::Mint::default())
        .expect("current time is an id instant")
}

pub struct Hub {
    pub(super) state: Mutex<HubState>,
    limits: Limits,

pub struct HubState {
    pub(super) rows: MemoryRows,
    identity: String,
    generation: u64,
    tables: BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    accounting: Accounting,
    version: Version,
    ring: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    preserved: VecDeque<(u64, Vec<String>)>,
    publisher: Option<Arc<dyn Fn(Snapshot) + Send + Sync>>,
}
impl HubState {
    fn new(identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) -> Self {
        Self {
            rows: MemoryRows::new(),
            identity,
            generation,
            tables: schema
                .tables
                .iter()
                .filter(|(_, t)| t.ephemeral.is_some())
                .map(|(n, t)| (n.clone(), t.clone()))
                .collect(),
            accounting: Accounting::default(),
            version: Version {
                epoch: 0,
                incarnation: incarnation(),
            },
            ring: VecDeque::new(),
            preserved: VecDeque::new(),
            publisher: None,
        }
    }
    pub fn version(&self) -> Version {
        self.version.clone()
    }
    pub fn snapshot(&self) -> Snapshot {
        Snapshot {
            identity: self.identity.clone(),
            generation: self.generation,
            version: self.version.clone(),
            ring: self.ring.clone(),
            preserved: self.preserved.clone(),
            tables: self.tables.keys().cloned().collect(),
        }
    }
    fn publish(&self) {
        if let Some(publish) = &self.publisher { publish(self.snapshot()); }
    }
    fn rebind(&mut self, identity: String, generation: u64, schema: &Schema) {
        let publisher = self.publisher.take();
        *self = Self::new(identity, generation, schema);
        self.publisher = publisher;
        self.publish();
    }

    pub fn bind(
        &mut self,
        identity: &str,
        generation: u64,
        schema: &Schema,
    ) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        if identity != self.identity || generation < self.generation {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
                "ephemeral hub serves a newer store or generation; retry with a fresh reader"
                    .into(),
