pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            if out.exhausted(){return;}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
                        continue 'actors;
                    }
                }
            }
            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
            if !reserve_observers(out,3){return;}
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world invalid: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(p,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}
                    let raw_args=args;
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&p.args,&raw_args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                    if args!=raw_args{
                        if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                        absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger fork: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                        if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger absence: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if let Err(why)=validate_fixture(bound,&mut absent){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: ledger absence world invalid: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if readback(&absent)!=Ok(absent_rows.clone()){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired argument ledger changes the absent base world",p.name));continue;}
                    }
                    out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}
                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: paired history observation is unstable or unavailable on repeat",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                    let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
                    if added&&let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
                    continue 'actors;
                }
            }
        }}
    }
}
fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
}
fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
            }
        }
    }
}
fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
    for inv in &bound.invariants{
pub(super) fn initial_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
    let Ok(mut engine)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return;};
    observe(bound,&mut engine,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    let probes=Vec::new();
    let mut groups=Vec::new();
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)){
        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,&initial,&actors,out);
        let mut group=relationship_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out);
        group.extend(role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out));
        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
        let mut seen=group.iter().map(|(_,viewer,args)|serde_json::to_string(&(viewer,args)).unwrap()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        // Give every actor the same first argument tuple before diagonal
        // variation; a referee must not see only the overturned bout by chance.
        if let Some(args)=cases.first(){for actor in &actors{
            if group.len()>=LIMIT||out.exhausted(){break;}
            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
        }}
        'diagonal:for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
            if cases.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){break 'diagonal;}
            if group.len()>=LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history candidate actors/arguments capped at 512",bp.program.name));break 'diagonal;}
            let actor=&actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()];let args=&cases[n%cases.len()];
            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
        }}
        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
    }
    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut candidate_keys=BTreeSet::new();
    // Front-load a small fair frontier of role and relationship changes so
    // ordinary no-op/rejected mutations cannot consume all observer work first.
    for group in &groups {
        let p=group[0].0;
        let priority=ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,kind)|*kind!="insert"&&(!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()||p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))));
        if priority{for c in group.iter().take(8){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}}}}
        if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}
    }
    'candidates:for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());}if candidates.len()==LIMIT{break 'candidates;}}}}
    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in initial.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){for viewer in &actors{
            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&initial,viewer,None,row,&mut base)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let mut witness=Campaign::default();violation(&mut witness,inv,&[],viewer,row,None,&initial,"always");
            if let Some(mut v)=witness.violations.pop(){v.reachability=Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace;v.operation="$fixture.assertion".into();v.rule_path="initial trusted fixture assertion; no authored mutation".into();v.scope="definite violation in authored fixture; initialization trusted, no authored transition claimed".into();out.violations.push(v);}break;
        }}
    }
    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
    // Spend half the pair budget on grant changes followed by an operation
    // as the affected principal; interleave first steps and successor names.
    let mut role_groups=Vec::new();
    for (i,(p,_,args)) in candidates.iter().enumerate().take(LIMIT){
        let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
        let rosters=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter_map(|(t,_)|{
            let roles=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&t);
            roles.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))).then_some(t)
        }).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        if rosters.is_empty(){continue;}
        let mut recipients=BTreeSet::new();
        for (name,kind) in &p.args {if let Some(value)=args.get(name){match kind {
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>{if let Value::Id(actor)=value{recipients.insert(actor.clone());}},
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rosters.contains(table)=>{
                if let Some(row)=initial.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(value)){
                    for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(actor))=row.field(column){recipients.insert(actor.clone());}}
                }
            },_=>{}
        }}}
        let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
        let successors=candidates.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(j,(next,actor,_))|(next.name!=p.name&&recipients.contains(actor)&&seen.insert((next.name.clone(),actor.clone()))).then_some((i,j))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if !successors.is_empty(){role_groups.push(successors);}
    }
    let mut role_pairs=Vec::new();
    'pairs:for n in 0..role_groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &role_groups{if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){role_pairs.push(*pair);if role_pairs.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'pairs;}}}}
    // Breadth first, then targeted and diagonal pairs: no first step owns the cap.
    let history_limit=if out.history_limit==0{LIMIT}else{out.history_limit.min(LIMIT)};
    let singles=candidates.len().min(history_limit);
    let mut setups=Vec::new();let mut role_changes=Vec::new();
    let mut setup_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut role_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut pair_plan=Vec::new();
    for n in 0..singles+history_limit {
        if !out.reserve(1){break;}
        if n==singles&&!candidates.is_empty(){
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            'setup:for &second in &role_changes{for &first in &setups{if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'setup;}}}}
            for &(first,second) in &role_pairs{if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT{break;}if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));}}
            let mut k=0;
            while pair_plan.len()<LIMIT{pair_plan.push((k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()));k+=1;}
        }
        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let (first,second)=pair_plan[n-singles];vec![first,second]};
        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
        for i in indices{
            if !out.reserve(1){break;}
            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){break;}match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
                Ok(result)=>{
                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
                    if sent&&n<singles{
                        let signature=serde_json::to_string(&(&p.name,viewer,&candidates[i].2)).unwrap();
                        let new_media=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"&&initial.get(&inv.table).is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)).any(|inv|{
                            let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|table.columns.iter().any(|(column,kind)|kind.contains_asset()&&row.field(column).is_ok_and(|v|*v!=Value::Null&&!matches!(v,Value::Array(xs) if xs.is_empty()))))
                        });
                        if new_media&&setup_seen.insert(signature.clone()){setups.push(i);}
                        let changed_role=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,_)|{
                            let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))
                        });
                        if changed_role&&role_seen.insert(signature){role_changes.push(i);}
                    }
                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
            }
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
}

//! A campaign fork retains activated semantics without running adoption again.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — isolation includes entropy, assets and ephemeral ownership.
    pub fn fork_fixture(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        if self.presence.is_some(){return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated external presence snapshot".into()));}
        let store=self.store.fork_memory()?;
        let identity=store.meta("store_id")?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture store has no identity".into()))?;
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule:timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral:std::sync::Arc::new(if self.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.ephemeral.is_some()){self.ephemeral.fork_fixture(&identity,self.generation)?}else{ephemeral::Hub::new(identity,self.generation,&self.schema)}),
            store,schema:self.schema.clone(),programs:self.programs.clone(),ceilings:self.ceilings,
            entropy:crate::ids::seed(),minter:crate::ids::Mint::default(),generation:self.generation,
            expiry_after:self.expiry_after.clone(),dev_effects:self.dev_effects,
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native:self.native.clone(),
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native_logs:native::Logs::default(),presence:None,
        })
    }
}

impl Engine {
    /// Judge independent fixture intent with the engine's authorized rule hops.
    pub fn fixture_policy(&mut self,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,next:&Value,now:i64)->Result<rules::Verdict,RuntimeError>{
        let tx=self.store.read_transaction()?;
        let context=if viewer==crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL{Context::system()}else{Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new())};
        let mut runner=Runner::new(&tx,&self.schema,context.at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(),Cut::Abort),0);
        let bytes=runner.rule_scratch(rule);
        runner.authorized_scratch(bytes,&[],|runner,allowance|{
            let mut probe=read::RuleProbe{runner,allowance};
            rules::evaluate_verdict(rule,viewer,old,next,false,&mut probe)
        })
    }
}

impl Engine {
    /// Test-only population through ordinary insertion, including field authority.
    /// The temporary program is private to this fixture engine and never adopted.
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — a row gate alone is not complete insertion authority.
    pub fn insert_fixture(&mut self,table:&str,row:Value,viewer:&str,now:i64)->Result<MutationOutcome,RuntimeError>{
        let name="$security.generated-insert";
        if viewer==crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL||self.programs.contains_key(name){return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture insertion requires an ordinary actor and a private probe name".into()));}
        self.programs.insert(name.into(),Program{name:name.into(),kind:crate::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation,args:BTreeMap::new(),body:vec![crate::ir::Stmt::Insert{table:table.into(),row:crate::ir::Expr::Literal(row)}],read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false});
        let result=self.mutate(name,None,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(now));
        self.programs.remove(name);
        result
    }
}

//! Concrete, isolated challenges; finite campaigns never establish proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — only replayed admission is a refutation.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Engine,Store,Context,Value,RuntimeError};
use snapback4_core::schema::{ColumnType as C,Constraint};
use snapback4_core::engine::rules::{self,Probe};

pub const NOW:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
const VIEWER:&str="dev:security-a";
const OTHER:&str="dev:security-b";
// Same verified 1x1 PNG header used by the existing packaged asset witnesses.

#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="kebab-case")]
pub enum Reachability { AuthoredFixtureTrace, GeneratedFixtureTrace, TrustedSeedOnly, SyntheticOperation }
impl Reachability {
    pub fn is_reachable(&self)->bool {matches!(self,Self::AuthoredFixtureTrace|Self::GeneratedFixtureTrace)}
}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Counterexample {pub reachability:Reachability,pub invariant_site:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub viewer:String,pub operation:String,pub effect:String,pub row:Value,pub old:Option<Value>,pub supporting_rows:BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,pub rule_path:String,pub scope:String, pub observations:Option<paired::Observations>,pub history:Vec<histories::Step>}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Default,Serialize)]
pub struct Campaign {#[serde(skip)] pub history_limit:usize,#[serde(skip)] pub generated_fixture:bool,#[serde(skip)] pub work_remaining:Option<usize>,#[serde(skip)] pub deadline:Option<std::time::Instant>,pub paired_observer_calls:usize,pub history_observer_calls:usize,pub column_observer_calls:usize,pub operations:BTreeMap<String,usize>,pub sequences:usize,pub attempted:usize,pub admitted:usize,pub refused:usize,pub violations:Vec<Counterexample>,pub boundary_failures:Vec<String>,pub unsupported:BTreeSet<String>}
impl Campaign {
    pub fn bounded(milliseconds:u64)->Self {Self{work_remaining:Some(512),deadline:Some(std::time::Instant::now()+std::time::Duration::from_millis(milliseconds)),..Self::default()}}
    pub(super) fn reserve(&mut self,cost:usize)->bool {
        if self.exhausted(){return false;}
        if let Some(remaining)=&mut self.work_remaining {
            if *remaining<cost{*remaining=0;self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return false;}
            *remaining-=cost;
        }true
    }
    pub(super) fn exhausted(&mut self)->bool {
        if self.work_remaining==Some(0){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration work budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());return true;}
        if self.deadline.is_some_and(|deadline|std::time::Instant::now()>=deadline){self.unsupported.insert("check concrete exploration time budget exhausted; remaining paths unproved; run snapback4 test for the full campaign".into());true}else{false}
    }
}
fn id(table:&str)->Value {Value::Id(format!("security-{table}"))}
fn sample(kind:&C,variant:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match kind{
    C::Optional(inner)=>if variant&4!=0{sample(inner,variant).unwrap_or(Value::Null)}else{Value::Null},C::Id|C::Principal=>Value::Id(if variant&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()),
    C::Ref{table}=>id(table),C::Text{max}=>Value::String("x".repeat((*max).min(20) as usize)),
    C::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(variant%variants.len())?.clone()),
    C::Int|C::Decimal{..}|C::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),C::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),
    C::Bool=>Value::Bool((variant>>2)&1==0),C::Time=>Value::Int(NOW-1000),C::Json{..}=>Value::Null,C::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),
    C::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),C::ListRange{of,min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|sample(of,variant)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    C::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),C::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),C::Video{..}=>return None,
})}
fn needed(schema:&Schema,name:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){
    if !out.insert(name.into()){return;}let Some(t)=schema.table(name)else{return};
    for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.target==name){needed(schema,&m.source,out);}
    for k in t.columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=k.required(){needed(schema,table,out);}}
    for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){needed(schema,&dep,out);}}
}
fn snapshot(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,false)
}
fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    });}
    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    let mut enum_case=variant;
    for name in names {
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&name];
        if bound.schema.maintain_for(&name).is_some(){continue;}
        let mut fields=BTreeMap::new();fields.insert("id".into(),id(&name));
        let mut principal_index=0usize;
        for (n,k) in &t.columns{
            if n=="id"||t.engine_field(n)||t.maintained.contains_key(n){continue;}
            let sample_variant=if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){let n=enum_case % variants.len();enum_case/=variants.len();n}else if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal){let n=((variant>>((if name==inv.table{0}else{3})+principal_index.min(5)))&1)|(variant&4);principal_index+=1;n}else{variant};
            let value=sample(k,sample_variant)
                .ok_or_else(||format!("{name}.{n}: asset ledger/required sample is not available"))?;
            fields.insert(n.clone(),value);
        }
        if timed || t.read_lifetime_sensitive() {for (n,k) in &t.columns {if matches!(k.required(),C::Time)&&(!k.is_optional()||variant&4!=0) {
            fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_ref()==Some(n){2000}else if timed{1000}else{-1000}));
        }}}
        for constraint in &t.constraints{if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=constraint
            && matches!(fields.get(field),Some(Value::String(_))) {fields.insert(field.clone(),Value::String("x".repeat((*min).max(1) as usize)));}}
        // Named primary keys used by role probes must select the challenged
        // viewer; changing the enum represents a current-role change while
        // derived assignment/membership rows remain present.
        if name!=inv.table {for (i,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,C::Principal)).enumerate(){fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Id(if variant & (1 << (i+3)) == 0 {VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into())); let _=k;}}
        out.insert(name,vec![Value::Row(fields.into())]);
    }
    Ok(out)
}
struct Snapshot<'a>{schema:&'a Schema,rows:&'a BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>}
impl Probe for Snapshot<'_>{
    fn exists(&mut self,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[Value],equalities:&[(String,Value)])->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};
        if idx.components.len()!=key.len(){return Ok(false);}
        if idx.unique&&idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column().is_some_and(|n|t.columns.get(n).is_some_and(C::is_optional)))&&key.contains(&Value::Null){return Ok(false);}
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|live(t,r)).any(|r|idx.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(c,v)|c.column().is_some_and(|n|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v)))&&equalities.iter().all(|(n,v)|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v))))
    }
    fn created(&mut self,_:&str,_:&str,_:&[Value])->bool{false}

}
fn live(t:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,row:&Value)->bool{
    !t.expired(row,NOW)&&t.not_before.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|match row.field(c){Ok(Value::Int(n))=>*n<=NOW,Ok(Value::Null)=>true,_=>false})
}
fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{

    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};

    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    if p.has_reference(){return Err("reference intent requires authorized engine policy replay".into());}
    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn policy_in(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<bool,String>{
    let Some(p)=&inv.policy else{return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);};
    if !p.has_reference(){return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);}
    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    match engine.fixture_policy(p,viewer,old,&image,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?{
        rules::Verdict::Readable=>Ok(true),rules::Verdict::Denied=>Ok(false),rules::Verdict::Unknown=>Err("policy reference/coverage judgment is unknown; never a refutation".into()),
    }
}
fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_with(bound,rows,vec![probe])
}
fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_on_store(bound,rows,probes,Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)
}
fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
        if inv.operation.is_some()||matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"){continue;}
        for p in &bound.programs {if p.program.kind!=ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}

        if combinations>512{out.unsupported.insert("schema enum Cartesian matrix exceeds 512 samples; remaining tuples are not covered".into());}
        for sample in 0..=combinations.min(512) {
            if !out.reserve(1){break;}

            // matrix. A value such as status='cancelled' need not wait behind
            // hundreds of unrelated enum tuples. This is only a candidate;
            // ordinary validation and engine replay still decide refutation.
            if sample==0 {
                fn pin(rule:&RuleExpr,row:&mut Value)->bool{match rule{
                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(v))|
                    RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Field(f))=>{
                        if let Value::Row(fields)=row&&fields.contains_key(f){fields.insert(f.clone(),v.clone());true}else{false}
                    },
                    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().fold(false,|any,r|pin(r,row)||any),
                    RuleExpr::Not(r)=>pin(r,row),_=>false,
                }}
                if !inv.policy.as_ref().zip(rows.get_mut(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first_mut())).is_some_and(|(p,row)|pin(p,row)){continue;}

            // Pruned dependencies can repeat after unrelated schema enum digits.
            // Identical seed/typed-argument candidates are one search input.

                    }if found{break;}continue;
                }
                if let Some(operation)=&inv.operation {

                                // a column contract cannot be refuted by editing a sibling.
                                if inv.target=="update"&&old.is_none() || inv.target=="insert"&&old.is_some() || inv.target=="delete" {continue;}

/// Fixture-free campaigns retain empty-start bootstraps and populated role worlds.
/// The two initial states share the existing 512-single/512-pair work envelope.
pub fn generated_campaign(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut Campaign,matrix:bool){
    for (name,_) in bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_some()){campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("ephemeral {name}: generated memory populations and ephemeral mutation histories remain uncovered; durable campaigns use isolated hub copies"));}
    campaign.history_limit=64;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return;}
    match engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new()){
        Ok(empty)=>fixture_observers(bound,&empty,campaign),
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("empty initial campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=448;campaign.generated_fixture=true;
    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
        Ok(fixture)=>if matrix{operations(bound,&fixture,campaign)}else{fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,campaign)},
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=0;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
}
/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    let first=campaign.violations.len();
    histories::initial_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if !campaign.generated_fixture {trusted_reads(bound,fixture,campaign);}
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if campaign.generated_fixture {for witness in &mut campaign.violations[first..] {
        if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace {witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated initial fixture; fixture initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}
    }}
}
pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    fixture_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
    let eligible=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)&&!p.program.native).count();
    let quota=(8192/eligible.max(1)).clamp(1,512);let mut remaining=8192usize;
    for p in &bound.programs{
        if !matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)||p.program.native{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: trusted/system/opaque operation",p.program.name));continue;}
        if ephemeral_operation(bound,&p.program,campaign){continue;}
        let target=bound.schema.visible_tables().find(|(t,_)|super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t)).or_else(||bound.schema.visible_tables().next()).map(|(t,_)|t.clone());
        let Some(target)=target else{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: no table population for generated arguments",p.program.name));continue;};
        let dummy=Invariant{table:target,target:"read".into(),column:None,predicate:"allow".into(),site:crate::Site::default(),policy:Some(RuleExpr::Allow),operation:Some(p.program.name.clone()),requirements:Vec::new(),syntax:None,unsupported:None};
        let cases=histories::fixture_cases(&p.program,&fixtures,&viewers,campaign);
        let mut exercised=0;
        'cases:for (variant,mut args) in cases.into_iter().enumerate(){
            let rows=if has_fixture{fixtures.clone()}else{match snapshot(bound,&dummy,variant){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));break;}}};
            if !has_fixture{for (arg,kind) in &p.program.args {if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind&&let Some(row)=rows.get(table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())&&let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(arg.clone(),id.clone());}}}
            for viewer in &viewers{
                if exercised==quota||remaining==0{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: operation matrix capped at {quota} calls to share the 8192-call budget across every operation; remaining arguments/viewers not covered",p.program.name));break 'cases;}
                exercised+=1;remaining-=1;
                let mut engine=match if has_fixture {fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())}else{engine(bound,&rows,program("$security.matrix",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]))}{Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {e}",p.program.name));continue;}};
                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: argument ledger unavailable: {why}",p.program.name));continue;}};
                campaign.attempted+=1;*campaign.operations.entry(p.program.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                if p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query{
                    match engine.query(&p.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(_)=>{},Err(e) if e.code=="E_INPUT"=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated arguments rejected as E_INPUT",p.program.name));},Err(_)=>campaign.refused+=1,}
                }else{
                    match engine.mutate(&p.program.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>campaign.refused+=1,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: matrix engine error {e}",p.program.name));},_=>{},}
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

    pub fn mutate(&mut self, name: &str, op: Option<&str>, mut context: Context) -> Result<MutationOutcome, RuntimeError> {
        context.generation = self.generation;
        let requested = context.now.unwrap_or_else(crate::now_ms);
        context.now = Some(self.advance_time_audience(requested)?);
        let wire_args = if op.is_some() { context.wire_args.take().unwrap_or_else(|| {
                serde_json::Value::Object(crate::wire::map_to_json(&context.args))
            }) } else { serde_json::Value::Null };
        if let Some(op) = op
            && let Some(outcome) = self.replay_mutation(&context.viewer, op, name, &wire_args)?
        {
            return Ok(outcome);
        }
        // An existing durable receipt owns its id even after conversion to ephemeral.
        if self.schema.operations.get(name).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral) {
            self.require_non_media_ephemeral(&context.viewer,op)?;
            self.schema.check_delivery(name, context.delivery_fingerprint.as_deref())?;
            let viewer=context.viewer.clone();
            return match self.mutate_ephemeral(name, context) {
                Ok(out) => Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Sent { seq: out.seq, result: out.result }, metered: out.metered, tables: out.ephemeral.tables, events: 0, replayed: false }),
                Err(error) if error.is_refusal() => Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why: seat_refusal(&self.schema,&error,&viewer), current: None }, metered: Metered::default(), tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false }),
                Err(error) => Err(error),
            };
        }
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        if let Some(op) = op
            && let Some(outcome) = crate::store::replay_bound(tx.conn(), &context.viewer, op, name, &wire_args)?
        {
            // Applied between the check above and this transaction.
            let outcome = MutationOutcome::from_receipt(&outcome)?;
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(outcome);
        }
        if let Some(op)=op{assets::require_placement(&tx,&context.viewer,op,name,&wire_args)?;}
        context.now = Some(time_audience::pin_writer_time(
            &self.schema,
            &tx,
            context.now.unwrap_or(0),
        )?);
        self.schema.check_delivery(name, context.delivery_fingerprint.as_deref())?;
        let program = self.programs.get(name).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::UnknownOp(name.to_owned()))?.clone();
        if program.kind == ProgramKind::Query {
            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is a query, not a mutation")));
        }
        if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is an action: call it with client.act")));
        }
        if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job {
            return Err(RuntimeError::UnknownOp(format!("{name} is a job: a schedule fires it, or `snapback4 run {name}` does; no caller can")));
        }
        if let Err(error) = validate::validate_inputs(&self.schema, &program, &mut context.args) {
            return Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why: error.refusal(), current: None }, metered: Metered::default(), tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false });
        }
        if context.presence.is_none() {
            context.presence = self.presence.clone();
        }
        context.entropy = self.entropy;
        context.minter = self.minter.clone();
        let ceilings = self.ceilings;
        let viewer = context.viewer.clone();
        let now = context.now.unwrap_or(0);
        // An `image`/`video` argument names an upload by id; the program
        // reads the facts the server established (`clip.duration`), so
        // they are put in place before it runs. An id that is not the
        // writer's own upload stays as sent and the write refuses it.
        for (name, kind) in &program.args {
            let inner = match kind {
                crate::ir::ArgType::Optional(inner) => inner.as_ref(),
                other => other,
            };
            if !matches!(inner, crate::ir::ArgType::Image { .. } | crate::ir::ArgType::Video { .. } | crate::ir::ArgType::File { .. }) {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(id) = asset_id(context.args.get(name)).map(str::to_owned) else { continue;
            };
            if let Some(asset) = tx.asset(&id)?
                && (asset.owner == viewer || context.is_system())
            {
                // @ref LLP 3000.038#3 — a file argument never reads an image's facts, nor an image argument a file's.
                if (asset.kind == AssetKind::File) != matches!(inner, crate::ir::ArgType::File { .. }) {
                    let wanted = if matches!(inner, crate::ir::ArgType::File { .. }) { "a file" } else { "an image or video" };
                    let error = RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE", message: format!("{name} takes {wanted} and {id} is {}; upload it as {wanted}", asset.kind.word()) };
                    return Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why: error.refusal(), current: None }, metered: Metered::default(), tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false });
                }
                context.args.insert(name.clone(), asset.column_value());
            }
        }
        tx.savepoint("program")?;
        let mut runner = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, context, Meter::new(ceilings, Cut::Abort), tx.next_seq()?);
        let ran = runner.run(&program).and_then(|value| {
            runner.charge_output(&value)?;
            Ok(value)
        });
        // Commit-end expiry can cascade or clear rows that an `after insert`
        // witness names. Final obligations therefore inspect the actual
        // provisional commit state, after every retirement action.
        let ran = ran.and_then(|value| runner.retire_expired_births().map(|_| value))
            .and_then(|value| runner.check_final_exists().map(|_| value));
        let metered = runner.meter.used;
        let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
        let events: Vec<Event> = runner.events.take();
        let action_tables = std::mem::take(&mut runner.action_tables);
        let outbox = std::mem::take(&mut runner.outbox);
        let rows_written = runner.rows_written;
        let mut read_tables = runner.read_tables.clone();
        self.entropy = runner.context.entropy;
        drop(runner);
        let outcome = match ran {
            Ok(result) => {
                tx.release("program")?;
                // @ref LLP 3000.019#4 — P7 reports keep at-most-once custody.
                // @ref LLP 3000.022#6 — a covered report keeps only a receipt at the committed head.
                let receipt_only = rows_written == 0 && outbox.is_empty()
                    && self.schema.delivery.iter().any(|binding| binding.mutation == name);
                let seq = tx.next_seq()? - u64::from(receipt_only);
                read_tables.extend(tables.iter().cloned());
                if !receipt_only {
                    tx.record_commit(seq, now, name, &viewer, program.kind.word(), &tables, rows_written)?;
                    partition::append(&tx, seq, &events)?;
                    effects::record(&tx, &outbox, seq, now, self.dev_effects, &self.schema)?;
                }
                MutationOutcome { receipt_only, state: WriteState::Sent { seq, result }, metered, tables, events: partition::disclosed_event_count(&self.schema, &events, &action_tables), replayed: false }
            }
            Err(error) if error.is_refusal() => {
                tx.rollback_to("program")?;
                let current = match &error {
                    RuntimeError::Conflict { current } => {
                        let mut probe = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, Context::new(viewer.clone(), BTreeMap::new()).at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::default(), Cut::Cap), 0);
                        let value = probe.get(current).ok();
                        value
                    }
                    _ => None,
                };
                let why=seat_refusal(&self.schema,&error,&viewer);
                MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why, current }, metered, tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false }
            }
            Err(error) => {
                tx.rollback()?;
                return Err(error);
            }
        };
        if let Some(op) = op {
            let seq = match &outcome.state {
                WriteState::Sent { seq, .. } => *seq,
                WriteState::Failed { .. } => tx.next_seq()? - 1,
            };
            tx.record_applied(&viewer, op, seq, &serde_json::to_string(&outcome.receipt(Some(op))).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?)?;
            tx.bind_applied(&viewer, op, name, &wire_args, now)?;
        }
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(outcome)
    }

    /// Write rows as the system principal: rules are bypassed, validation,
    /// constraints, aggregates and partition events are not. One commit.
    pub fn seed(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.seed_at(rows, crate::now_ms())
    }

    /// Development hosts supply their clock so seed commits share operation and stream time.
    pub fn seed_at(&mut self, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>, now: i64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        let now = self.advance_time_audience(now)?;
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let count = seed_rows(&self.schema, &tx, rows, now)?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(count)
    }

    /// Delete a row as the system principal: constraints, aggregates and
    /// partition events apply. Returns the number of rows removed (0 or 1).
    pub fn seed_delete(&mut self, table: &str, id: &str) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.seed_delete_at(table,id,crate::now_ms())
    }
    /// Trusted deletion with the fixture/host clock, including publication and expiry.
    pub fn seed_delete_at(&mut self, table: &str, id: &str, now:i64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.schema.refuse_ephemeral_import(table)?;
        timeline::check_import(&self.schema, table)?;
        let now = self.advance_time_audience(now)?;
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
        let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
        let mut runner = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(if crate::schema::live::has_live(&self.schema) {Ceilings::job()}else{Ceilings::unbounded()}, Cut::Abort), seq);
        if crate::schema::live::has_live(&self.schema) {runner.reserve_bulk_source(table)?;}
        let removed = match runner.delete(table, id) {
            Ok(()) => 1,
            Err(RuntimeError::Require { .. }) => 0,
            Err(error) => return Err(error),
        };
        let events = runner.events.take();
        let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
        drop(runner);
        if removed > 0 {
            tx.record_commit(seq, now, "seed", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "seed", &tables, removed)?;
            partition::append(&tx, seq, &events)?;
        }
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(removed)
    }

/bin/bash -lc "timeout 20s git show c4a21db:llp/3000.017-ephemeral-tables.rfc.md | sed -n '560,740p'" in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4
 succeeded in 0ms:

**`GET /changes` parameters.**
- `eph=<epoch>`.
- `ephi=<incarnation>`.
- `ephTables=<comma-separated table names>`:
  - absent means "wake me for every ephemeral commit";
  - present, possibly empty, means "wake me only for these tables".
- Clients send all three when the backend declares an ephemeral table.
  A client with no ephemeral interest sends `ephTables=` (empty). A
  durable-only backend keeps the old wire: clients omit all three, with no
  initial incarnation reset (§3.6).
- An online client without schema facts learns support from
  `X-Snapback-Ephemeral: 1` on ordinary responses/heartbeats, present only for
  ephemeral backends and exposed through CORS. It loads `/schema` and reissues
  any legacy discovery poll immediately. Generated read facts can enable the
  parameters before discovery. This preserves the entire durable-only request
  flow, including clients whose transport has no `/schema` endpoint.

**When the poll answers.** Exactly as today, plus two conditions:
- the store's sequence moved past `since`;
- the store identity changed;
- the generation differs from the generation at poll entry;
- the wait expired;
- a table in its interest was written after `eph` (new);
- the incarnation differs from `ephi` (new).

**Wake filtering and pacing.**
- A wake caused only by an ephemeral write outside the interest keeps
  waiting. It neither answers nor restarts the heartbeat.
- If the ring no longer covers `eph`, the answer names every table in the
  interest.
- An answer caused only by ephemeral writes is not sent sooner than 100 ms
  after the poll began. That bounds each client to 10 ephemeral-only
  re-polls a second, however fast a session writes.

**What the answer carries.**
- It gains `"ephemeral": { "epoch": n, "incarnation": "…", "tables": [...] }`.
- A poll with none of the three parameters answers exactly as before (the
  field omitted), apart from waking for ephemeral commits. A schema with no
  ephemeral table has none, so for it this is byte-identical to today.

**Re-issuing a poll.**
- Backend adoption reconciles every mounted watch against the adopted
  operation facts, including additions and removals. The client re-issues its
  held poll whenever its ephemeral interest set grows (§2.4).
- The re-issued poll's `eph` is the smallest epoch among the current
  incarnation's latest answers of its ephemeral watches.
- A watch with no answer yet contributes the client's last `/changes`
  epoch.
- A write that lands between an answer and the new poll is therefore always
  after `eph`, and the new poll answers at once.

**Query answers.**
- A query answer that read an ephemeral table carries
  `"ephemeral": { "epoch": n, "incarnation": "…" }`.
- The epoch is read under the hub lock, in the same critical section as the
  memory rows.

**Fencing.**
- The client keeps an **ephemeral revision**, a counter it tags onto every
  ephemeral query request and `/changes` poll it sends.
- Accepting a new incarnation advances the revision. The client then
  invalidates its outstanding ephemeral queries and its poll, and re-issues
  them.
- A response tagged with an older revision is dropped before its
  incarnation is looked at. So `A:50 → B:1 → delayed A:50` cannot reinstate
  the retired incarnation A. (Revision 3: under revision 2's rule, a
  different incarnation was always accepted. Astra's protocol model
  reproduced the resurrection.)
- Within the current revision, each watch remembers its own
  `(incarnation, epoch)` floor.
- An answer from a different incarnation is accepted, advances the
  revision, and replaces the floor.
- An answer from the same incarnation is accepted only if its epoch is at
  least the floor.
- This is independent of durable `seq` fencing (`minimumReadSeq`), and the
  two are never folded together.
- It applies in the online tier's `currentRead`, in `local-query.ts`'s
  publication path, and to `/changes` answers before they are judged stale.

**Presence re-polls.** Poll entry today runs `presence_reconnected`, which
opens an IMMEDIATE transaction (`engine/effects.rs:287-291`). With this
change, it first checks without a write transaction whether the principal
has a pending presence deferral. It opens the write transaction only if it
has one. The effect semantics are unchanged; ephemeral re-polls stop paying
a writer transaction each.

### 3.4 Generation fence, restart, adoption

The memory store is bound to the `(store identity, generation)` it serves.

- Every hub access runs under the hub lock, for mutations and for queries.
  It compares the durable snapshot's `(store identity, generation)`, which
  its read transaction has already established, with the hub's binding:
  - **hub older:** clear it, rebind to the snapshot's pair, and change the
    incarnation;
  - **snapshot older:** refuse with the store's retryable generation
    refusal. The request retries on a fresh reader.
- Adoption (`serve_adoption.rs`) holds the engine mutex. In-place activation
  checks the hub for poison, releases it throughout the SQLite transaction,
  then takes it to clear and rebind after commit. It never holds the hub while
  waiting for SQLite readers: those readers take SQLite before the hub, and a
  DELETE-mode writer needs them to finish before commit or spill. A hub poisoned
  after the initial check is completely reset at committed rebind, avoiding a
  post-commit refusal (LLP 3000.033 §1.3). Engine replacement (`open_engine_at`)
  likewise clears and rebinds the shared hub before publishing the new engine.
- A reader loaned before adoption can therefore never write under the old
  generation's indexes into the new store, nor read new rows under old
  rules.

Ephemeral rows do not survive any of these:
- a server restart;
- schema adoption under `dev`;
- a generation change;
- a store replacement.

Each changes the incarnation, so every client watching an ephemeral table
re-runs its queries (§3.3 fencing).

Day-two classification (`successor.rs`):
- **Destructive:** a durable table gaining `ephemeral`. Activation deletes
  its stored rows and index entries even though the table name remains
  (`store/activation.rs` today deletes only disappeared names).
- **Additive:**
  - an ephemeral table becoming durable, as a new, empty table;
  - adding an ephemeral table;
  - removing one;
  - changing one.

The ephemeral case must be decided before the generic "removed; its rows
are lost" branch (`successor.rs:65-69`). The `$deadline` engine-field
branch is not the only step.

### 3.5 Bounds

| Bound | Value | Refusal |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Life | 1 s – 60 s | `E_QUARRY_EPHEMERAL` at `check`; generation admission re-checks |
| Live rows written by one principal | 1,000 | `E_EPHEMERAL_FULL` |
| Declared bytes written by one principal | 4 MiB | `E_EPHEMERAL_FULL` |
| Live rows, all principals (backstop) | 100,000 | `E_EPHEMERAL_FULL` |
| Declared bytes, all principals (backstop) | 512 MiB | `E_EPHEMERAL_FULL` |
| Ephemeral mutations per session | 20 per second sustained, burst 40 | `E_RATE_LIMIT` |
| Waiting calls in a client's lane | 256 | `E_RATE_LIMIT` (local) |
| Rows per group | the table's `cap … by …` | the existing cap refusal |
| Work per mutation and per query | the existing meter | unchanged |

Every refusal in this table is retryable, carries no row detail, and
arrives as HTTP 200 `{ state: "failed", why }` (§2.4 rule 5).

**How capacity is counted.**
- The hub records each row's writing principal.
- A row is charged its **declared maximum image size**, computed from the
  schema's bounds for its columns and index keys, never its actual bytes.
- Per-principal admission therefore depends only on the writer's own rows
  and on the schema. Another principal's private payload cannot flip it
  (Astra F3).
- The global backstop reveals only a server-wide aggregate. That is the
  same residual class as HTTP admission being full.

**When capacity is checked.**
- Capacity counts live rows only. The hub sweeps expired rows before it
  refuses.
- The check runs under the hub lock, after the program and before the epoch
  bump. A refusal undoes the journal: the row is absent and the epoch does
  not move.

**The rate bucket is new.**
- It is a new per-session token bucket, checked before the mutation runs.
- It applies under `dev` and `serve` alike.
- It shares nothing with the login and upload counters that `E_RATE_LIMIT`
  names today. The code is reused, its card text is extended, and its
  family stays `auth`.

The lane's coalescing and renewal window keep an ordinary typing indicator
near one request per second per typist. That is far below these bounds.

### 3.6 What does not change, and what the lock claims are

**What does not change.**
- A schema with no ephemeral table serializes byte-identically. Its wire,

    if word=="update" {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let changed=table.columns.keys().filter(|c|!table.engine_field(c)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(*c))
            .any(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()&&inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|column|column==c));
        if !changed{return Err("sent no-op/implicit-only update has no changed declared target column".into());}
    }
    Ok((true,next))
}
fn readback(engine:&Engine)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{engine.schema().tables.keys().map(|t|engine.store().rows(t).map(|rs|(t.clone(),rs)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).collect()}

fn operation_preflight(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<(),String>{
    let Some(name)=&inv.operation else{return Ok(());};
    let p=&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==name).ok_or("operation did not bind")?.program;
    if !super::census::body(&p.body){return Err(format!("{name}: opaque/loop/scan path requires write-boundary tracing"));}
    let rule=inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("operation policy outside rule fragment")?;
    if rule.any_term(&|t|!matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))){return Err("operation policy requires write-boundary row-image tracing".into());}
    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
    if writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).count()>1{return Err(format!("{name}: multiple target writes need executed-effect tracing; net images do not identify delete/reinsert"));}
    let unstable=kernel::dependencies(rule).iter().any(|dep|{
        let hits=writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==dep).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")
    });
    if unstable||!bound.schema.maintains.is_empty()||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.sequences.is_empty()||!t.maintained.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())){
        return Err("operation policy dependency may change before/after its write boundary; snapshot correspondence unproved".into());
    }Ok(())
}

/// Schema-only engine challenges. Successful synthetic operations refute the
/// declared table-authority contract, not application-operation reachability.
pub fn run(bound:&Bound)->Campaign{run_selected(bound,None)}
/// `check` searches unproved direct obligations; unsearched blockers remain
/// explicitly unproved. Default `test` passes None and runs the whole set.
pub fn run_selected(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>)->Campaign{run_selected_after(bound,sites,Campaign::default())}
/// Continue after trusted observations, preserving one set of work budgets.
pub fn run_selected_after(bound:&Bound,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>,mut out:Campaign)->Campaign{
    // A direct component theorem needs no repeated concrete search for that
    // same component. Indirect observers, temporal reads and actual authored
    // operations below still run; the whole declaration remains unproved.
    let discharged=super::analyze(bound).invariants.into_iter()
        .filter(|v|v.direct_rule==Status::Proved&&v.invariant.operation.is_none()
            && (matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"read"|"always"|"insert"|"delete")
                || matches!(v.invariant.target.as_str(),"update"|"write")&&bound.schema.tables[&v.invariant.table].written_by.is_empty()))
        .map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let mut assertions=bound.invariants.clone();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.target=="read"&&inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue;}
            if found{break;}
        }
    }
    column_reads::generated(bound,&mut out,sites);
    paired::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    timed::run(bound,&mut out,sites);
    out.unsupported.insert("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require a live server; engine principals are authenticated contexts".into());
    out.unsupported.insert("offline/revoked-device access and retained history require device boundary qualification".into());out
}
pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){
    report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());
    let rank=|c:&Counterexample|match c.reachability{Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace=>0,Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace=>1,_=>2};
    let committed=|c:&Counterexample|c.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    for c in &campaign.violations {for v in &mut report.invariants {if v.invariant.site.to_string()!=c.invariant_site{continue;}
        if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|rank(old)<rank(c)||rank(old)==rank(c)&&committed(old)<=committed(c)){continue;}
        v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());
        v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; fixture origin is explicit, not an empty-state reachability theorem".into());
    }}
}

fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    use ir::ArgType as A;
/// Fixture-free campaigns retain empty-start bootstraps and populated role worlds.
/// The two initial states share the existing 512-single/512-pair work envelope.
pub fn generated_campaign(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut Campaign,matrix:bool){
    for (name,_) in bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_some()){campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("ephemeral {name}: generated memory populations and ephemeral mutation histories remain uncovered; durable campaigns use isolated hub copies"));}
    campaign.history_limit=64;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return;}
    match engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new()){
        Ok(empty)=>fixture_observers(bound,&empty,campaign),
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("empty initial campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=448;campaign.generated_fixture=true;
    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
        Ok(fixture)=>if matrix{operations(bound,&fixture,campaign)}else{fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,campaign)},
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=0;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
}
/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    let first=campaign.violations.len();
    histories::initial_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if !campaign.generated_fixture {trusted_reads(bound,fixture,campaign);}
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if campaign.generated_fixture {for witness in &mut campaign.violations[first..] {
        if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace {witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated initial fixture; fixture initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}
    }}
}
pub fn operations(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    fixture_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    assets::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    let fixtures=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    let has_fixture=fixtures.values().any(|r|!r.is_empty());
    if fixtures.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{campaign.unsupported.insert("operation fixture matrix exceeds 1000 rows; provide a focused security fixture".into());return;}
    let mut viewers=histories::viewers(bound);
    for (table,rows) in &fixtures{for row in rows{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!viewers.contains(p){viewers.push(p.clone());}}}}
    let eligible=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,ProgramKind::Query|ProgramKind::Mutation)&&!p.program.native).count();
    let quota=(8192/eligible.max(1)).clamp(1,512);let mut remaining=8192usize;
    for p in &bound.programs{
