// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — explicit saved bytes replace only source selection, never activation or store semantics.
use super::{Options, Project};
use std::path::Path;

/// Select one complete saved backend, or the ordinary multi-file project.
/// The vector is retained for both compilation and staging: one read, no swap.
pub(super) fn sources(project: &Project, options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, String> {
    match options.flags.get(flag) {
        None => project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Some(file) => {
            let path = Path::new(file);
            if !path.try_exists().map_err(|e|format!("cannot inspect saved source {file}: {e}"))? {return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist"));}
            if path.is_dir() {
                return crate::client_compatibility::sources(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
            }
            let name = path.file_name().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).ok_or("saved source needs a UTF-8 file name")?;
            if !name.ends_with(".q") { return Err("saved source must be a complete Quarry .q file".into()); }
            let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound {format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist")} else {format!("cannot read saved source {file}: {e}")})?;
            if text.trim().is_empty() { return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved Quarry source is empty")); }
            // A saved complete backend retains the schema namespace regardless of its archive file name.
            Ok(vec![("schema.q".into(), text)])
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)], options: &Options, flag: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get(flag) {
        for diagnostic in &mut bound.diagnostics { diagnostic.site.file = if Path::new(file).is_dir() { Path::new(file).join(&diagnostic.site.file).to_string_lossy().into_owned() } else { file.clone() }; }
    }
    bound
}

/// A saved file can be used outside a project. Companion code is selected
/// only from an actual current project, never from arbitrary sibling files.
pub(super) fn project(options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Project, String> {
    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if let Some(project) = Project::locate(&cwd) { return Ok(project); }
    if options.flags.contains_key(flag) { return Ok(Project { root: cwd }); }
    Err("no snapback/*.q here or above; run `snapback4 init` or select a saved source file".into())
}

/// A private companion build uses the selected schema's generated contracts.
/// Packaging still records the original host installation, never this scratch.
pub(super) struct Candidate { pub project: Project, cleanup: std::path::PathBuf }
impl Drop for Candidate { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.cleanup); } }
impl Candidate {
    pub(super) fn prepare(original: &Project, stage: &Path, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound) -> Result<Self, String> {
        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
        static SERIAL: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-saved-source-{}-{}", std::process::id(), SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        #[cfg(unix)] snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut candidate = Self { project: Project { root: root.clone() }, cleanup: root };
        candidate.project.root = crate::dev_worker::private_scope(original, &candidate.cleanup)?;
        // The worker snapshotter captures the actual import closure and refuses
        // symlinks. Copy those captured bytes, not an unbounded application tree.
        copy_tree(stage, &candidate.project.root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        // Discover the current dependency graph without installing selected
        // contracts in the working project. A failed typecheck still publishes
        // its graph; the copied bytes are checked with selected contracts below.
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(original) {
            let _ = original.check_entry(&entry);
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        }
        candidate.project.write_generated_from(bound, original).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if !crate::native::declared(bound).is_empty() && original.native_manifest().exists() {
            copy_tree(&original.native_dir(), &candidate.project.native_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            rebase_tree(&candidate.project.native_dir(), &original.native_dir()).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native snapshot: {e}"))?;
        }
        crate::dev_worker::link_dependencies(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(&candidate.project) { candidate.project.check_entry(&entry)?; }
        Ok(candidate)
    }
}
fn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(target)?;
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)? {
        let entry = entry?;
        if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(), Some("target" | "native-target" | ".git")) { continue; }
        let kind = entry.file_type()?;
        if kind.is_dir() { copy_tree(&entry.path(), &target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else if kind.is_file() { std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else { return Err(std::io::Error::other("saved-source companion snapshot permits regular source files only")); }
    }
    Ok(())
}


#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn dir(label: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-saved-{}-{label}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root); std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/generated")).unwrap(); root
    }
    #[test]
    fn archived_names_preserve_operations_and_diagnostics_name_the_selected_input() {
        let root = dir("archived"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        let file = root.join("v1.q");
        std::fs::write(&file, "query alive():\n  return true\n").unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], flags: [("schema".into(), file.to_str().unwrap().into())].into(), json: false };
        let selected = sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(selected[0].0, "schema.q");
        let bound = compile(&selected, &options, "schema");
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert_eq!(bound.programs[0].program.name, "alive");
        std::fs::write(&file, "not a Quarry backend").unwrap();
        let bound = compile(&sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap(), &options, "schema");
        assert!(!bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().all(|d| d.site.file == file.to_str().unwrap()));
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn missing_baseline_names_original_absolute_location() {
        let root = dir("missing-baseline"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::write(root.join("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), r#"{"baseline":"versions/v1","since_release":2}"#).unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "query alive():\n  return true\n".into())]);
        let error = project.write_generated(&bound).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(error.contains(root.join("versions/v1").to_str().unwrap()) && error.contains("does not exist (resolved from") && error.contains("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), "{error}");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn inherited_native_workspace_refuses_before_touching_working_generated_types() {
        let root = dir("workspace"); let stage = dir("workspace-stage");
        let original = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::create_dir_all(original.native_dir()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), "[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion.workspace = true\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_types(), "working sentinel").unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "job sweep(): native\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let error = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).err().unwrap();
        assert!(error.contains("workspace-inherited") && error.contains("standalone"), "{error}");
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap(); std::fs::remove_dir_all(stage).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn native_types_are_built_for_selected_source_in_private_tree() {
        let root = dir("native"); let stage = dir("stage");
        let original = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::create_dir_all(original.native_dir().join("src")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), "[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion = \"0.0.0\"\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_types(), "working sentinel").unwrap();
        let source = "table notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  color: text <=20\n  public 'shared'\njob sweep(): native\n";
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("v2.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let candidate = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).unwrap();
        crate::native::build(&candidate.project, &bound).err().expect("private build refuses absent native target"); // no lib target: a private build refusal, never working generated writes
        assert!(std::fs::read_to_string(candidate.project.native_types()).unwrap().contains("color"));
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        let manifest = "[dependencies]\nsdk = { path = \"../sdk\" }\n";
        std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/sdk")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), manifest).unwrap();
        let relocated = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).unwrap();
        let parsed: toml::Value = std::fs::read_to_string(relocated.project.native_manifest()).unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(parsed["dependencies"]["sdk"]["path"].as_str().unwrap(), root.join("snapback/sdk").canonicalize().unwrap().to_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_manifest()).unwrap(), manifest);
        drop(relocated);
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        drop(candidate); std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap(); std::fs::remove_dir_all(stage).unwrap();
    }
}

fn rebase_native(text: &str, original: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<String, String> {
    let mut manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))?;
    fn inherited(value: &toml::Value) -> bool {
        let marker = |field: &toml::Value| field.get("workspace").and_then(toml::Value::as_bool) == Some(true);
        if value.get("package").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.iter().any(|(key, field)| key != "metadata" && marker(field))) { return true; }
        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies"] {
            if value.get(key).and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.values().any(marker)) { return true; }
        }
        value.get("target").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|targets| targets.values().any(inherited))
    }
    if !workspace && inherited(&manifest) { return Err("workspace-inherited native packages require a complete standalone [workspace] under snapback/native; copy its workspace package/dependency declarations before saved-source deployment".into()); }
    fn dependency_group(group: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        if let Some(dependencies) = group.as_table_mut() {
            for (_, dependency) in dependencies.iter_mut() {
                if let Some(path) = dependency.as_table_mut().and_then(|fields| fields.get_mut("path")) {
                    let relative = path.as_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be a string")?;
                    let absolute = root.join(relative).canonicalize().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native dependency {relative}: {e}"))?;
                    let absolute = absolute.strip_prefix(source_root).map(|relative| target_root.join(relative)).unwrap_or(absolute.clone());
                    *path = toml::Value::String(absolute.to_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be UTF-8")?.to_owned());
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn groups(table: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies", "replace"] {
            if let Some(group) = table.get_mut(key) { dependency_group(group, root, source_root, target_root)?; }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    groups(&mut manifest, original, source_root, target_root)?;
    for category in ["target", "patch"] {
        if let Some(table) = manifest.get_mut(category).and_then(toml::Value::as_table_mut) {
            for (_, item) in table.iter_mut() {
                if category == "target" { groups(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
                else { dependency_group(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
            }
        }
    }
    if let Some(workspace) = manifest.get_mut("workspace") { groups(workspace, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
    toml::to_string(&manifest).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))
}

fn rebase_tree(target: &Path, original: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(target.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("native manifest: {e}"))?;
    let workspace = manifest.get("workspace").is_some();
    fn walk(directory: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(directory).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            let entry = entry.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if entry.file_type().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_dir() { walk(&entry.path(), source_root, target_root, workspace)?; }
            else if entry.file_name() == "Cargo.toml" {
                let relative = entry.path().strip_prefix(target_root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.to_owned();
                let source = source_root.join(relative).parent().unwrap().to_owned();
                let text = std::fs::read_to_string(entry.path()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                let rebased = rebase_native(&text, &source, source_root, target_root, workspace)?;
                std::fs::write(entry.path(), rebased).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    let original = original.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    walk(target, &original, target, workspace)
}

fn report(verb: &str, result: Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — release completion owns no engine/lease; bound the final diagnostic drain.
    // The background writer must get a chance to emit adoption estimates before
    // this short-lived command exits. Broken/stalled output cannot hold exit.
    let _ = snapback4_core::diagnostics::drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3));
    match result {
        Ok(()) => 0,
        Err(error) => {
            eprintln!("{verb}: {error}");
            1
        }
    }
}

pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    report(
        "deploy",
        (|| {
            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
            let _deployment = if checking {None} else {target(&dir)?};
            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
            let bound = compiled_or_exit(super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"), options.json);
            let mut saved_version = super::saved_version::SavedVersion::prepare(&project,&sources,options,&dir)?;
            if options.flags.contains_key("staging") {
                staging::classify(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), existed, checking)?;
            }
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("releases")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let held = if checking {
                if dir.join("store.sqlite").exists() {snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).and_then(|mut s|s.release_status(0,None,None)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?}
                else {serde_json::json!({"release":null,"revision":0,"generation":1})}
            } else {status(&dir, _deployment.as_ref(), 0, None, None)?};
            let previous = if let Some(owner) = _deployment.as_ref() {
                current(&dir, owner)?
            } else {
                held["release"].as_u64()
            };
            {
                let prior=previous.map(|n|bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))).transpose()?;
                super::security::release_loop(&project,&bound,&sources,prior.as_ref(),options);
            }
            let revision = held["revision"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
            check_change(&dir, previous, &bound, options, None)?;
            let releases = dir.join("releases").canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let stage = releases.join(format!(".stage-{}", std::process::id()));
            if stage.exists() {std::fs::remove_dir_all(&stage).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
            std::fs::create_dir(&stage).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let _cleanup=Stage(stage.clone());
            std::fs::create_dir(stage.join("snapback")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let source_hash = {
                use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
                let mut hash = Sha256::new();
                let mut ordered = sources.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
                ordered.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
                for (name,text) in ordered {
                    hash.update((name.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(name.as_bytes());
                    hash.update((text.len() as u64).to_le_bytes()); hash.update(text.as_bytes());
                }
                format!("{:x}", hash.finalize())
            };
            for (name,text) in sources {
                let path=stage.join("snapback").join(name);
                if let Some(parent)=path.parent(){std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;}
                std::fs::write(path,text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            }
            crate::dev_worker::package(&project, &stage)?;
            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — saved schemas prepare companion types in a private snapshot, never in working generated/.
            let private = if options.flags.contains_key("schema") || crate::dev_worker::source(&project).is_some() {
                Some(super::proof_source::Candidate::prepare(&project, &stage, &bound)?)
            } else { None };
            let build_project = private.as_ref().map(|candidate| &candidate.project).unwrap_or(&project);
            // A native job's module travels with the captured source.
            if let Some(built) =
                crate::native::build(build_project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?
            {
                let generated = stage.join("snapback").join("generated");
                std::fs::create_dir_all(&generated).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                std::fs::copy(&built.path, generated.join("native.wasm"))
                    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
            std::fs::write(stage.join("device-compatibility.json"), serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection())).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(build_project, &stage)?;

### E_SECURITY_TARGET

E_SECURITY_TARGET means the invariant target does not name a declared table, column or effect. Correct the target name; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_SECURITY_INTENT

N_SECURITY_INTENT means access rules lack independent declared intent. Declare policy from the spec; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_BROAD_READ

N_BROAD_READ identifies universal or roster-wide readership; its message states the exact condition. Confirm the intended audience; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE

N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE means a stored grant may survive a later role change. Recheck the current reader role; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_ARGUMENT_GRANT

N_ARGUMENT_GRANT means an argument becomes a reader-grant column. Validate the recipient role and relationship; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE

N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE means optional media permits omitted evidence. Declare required media when the spec requires it; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE

N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE means sweeps leave time between a deadline and cleanup. Enforce the deadline in the mutation; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

### N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE

N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE means absence and later denial can reveal different outcomes. Unify the code and message when existence is private; inspect `audit` and the policy before changing a rule.

The isolated production HTTP campaign also signs in, refreshes the session,
logs out all sessions and retries both retired credentials against query,
mutation, schema, changes, sync, probe and refresh doors. It reports missing coverage
when ordinary identity or complete deployed worker artifacts are unavailable.
This checks server admission; a disconnected device can still retain old bytes.

### N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION

N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION identifies self-signup roles or role-grant operations that can confer authority trusted by other rules. Restrict assignment to authorized delegation; provision the first administrator with `guide staging`.

Identity rate refusals return HTTP `Retry-After` (seconds) and
`denied.retryAfterMs` (milliseconds), conservatively covering the 60-second
window. Wait that interval before retrying identity requests.

REL5 owns release transitions/old-client compatibility; MIG owns backfills and
staging clock control for day-two qualification.

### N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE

N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE means intent and the effective rule admit the same cases
in the bounded predicate kernel. Check the original requirement before rewriting; an equivalent policy
may be correct, but cannot detect a mistake copied into both declarations.

### N_ROLE_WIDE_READ

N_ROLE_WIDE_READ means a role-wide branch gated by a row value exposes every column
on matching rows.

notes, principals, images and files separately.

# Rules

**Snapback 2 optimizes the edit-to-verified loop — the agent's building an app on it, and ours building it.**

Speed is the constraint. Everything below exists to keep the edit-to-verified loop short
and to stop this repository accreting apparatus faster than anyone can remove it. These
rules bind `snapback2/` and the LLPs numbered 1000 and up; Snapback 1 is baselined, not
exempt, and its numbers may only fall.

Each rule is **[check]** (enforced by `caps`) or **[review]** (enforced by a human).

## Budgets — every budget is a trade, not a limit

- **5 blocking checks, 60s total.** A sixth requires deleting one, in the same PR. **[check]**
- **59 documents in the working set** (`llp/current/`). Today's count. A 60th requires
  archiving one — which is just removing the link. Snapback 2 aims to leave this at 15;
  Snapback 1's links are archived at promotion. **[check]**
- **12 documents in the foundation** (`llp/foundation/`). **[check]**
- **This file: 700 words.** If it grows, something becomes a check or stops being a rule. **[check]**
- **1,500 lines per source file, baseline 204.** Generated files exempt — and generated
  files are built, not committed; so are benchmark run records under `benchmarks/runs/`,
  which are evidence, not source. The baseline is Snapback 1's oversize count on the day
  the rule landed; it may not grow, and `snapback2/` contributes zero to it. **[check]**

## Blocking checks

`caps` · `ref-check` · `snapback2/scripts/test.sh` · `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs --check` · `check-inventories`

## Loop shape

- **Every check reports all failures in one run.** Fail-fast is banned. **[check]**
- **Nothing blocks on anything slower than 60 seconds.** Slow verification runs on a
  schedule, attributed to the breaking commit, and files a bounded set of issues. **[review]**
- **Fix loops get 3 rounds.** Then stop: escalate to a human or descope. **[review]**

## Time budgets

Tracked every run of `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs` (appends `snapback2/metrics/loop.jsonl`).
A regression is a P0 with a name on it.

| | budget |
|---|---|
| Incremental rebuild after a one-line edit | 15s |
| Test what you just changed | 30s |
| Blocking gate suite | 60s |
| Full build, warm cache | 5 min |
| **`snapback2 check` on a program (the agent's product latency)** | **1s** |
| **`snapback2 doctor` boot-to-ready** | **0.5s** |

## Scope

- **`NOT-DOING.md` is binding.** Moving something onto the doing-list means writing why
  and taking something off. **[review]**
- **Delete; don't deprecate.** No compat shims or migration paths before promotion. **[review]**
- **Nothing is compiled, transpiled, or generated at runtime that could be built ahead of
  time.** **[review]**
- **A spec needs an implementer and a date, or it isn't written.** **[review]**
- **Only implemented specs bind.** A document with no running code behind it is a
  proposal, and a proposal cannot block a PR or justify a check. **[review]**

## Agents

- **Agents remove apparatus freely and add none.** An agent PR that adds a check, script,
  registry, config file, or design doc needs explicit human approval saying so. **[review]**
- **2026-09-06 session exception:** the user delegated the search implementation
  decisions; one `snapback2-core/build.rs` may generate pinned Unicode tables at
  build time, alongside the existing package build. No new gate, registry,
  runtime generator, or Unicode dependency is authorized by this exception. **[review]**
- **Snapback 2 LLPs are numbered 1000 and up**; `llp-create` must be told. **[review]**
- **Snapback 4 LLPs are numbered 3000 and up** (`snapback4/`, LLP 3000); it adopts these budgets
  and loop shape as its own and keeps its scope in `snapback4/NOT-DOING.md`. **[review]**

## Shared machines

- Warm, named lanes, one per stream of work. Never clone fresh; reuse the worktree.
- **Never `git stash`.** Use a temporary commit.
- Kill only PIDs you recorded at launch. Never by name or pattern.

# LLP 2000.000: Snapback 3 — keep the core, flip two substrates, start at hub scale

**Type:** RFC
**Status:** Draft
**Role:** Second opinion on LLP 2000; since 2026-09-12 also the design brief Snapback 4 builds (LLP 3000)
**Systems:** Snapback 3 product architecture, bounded computation, client data model, client API vocabulary, language and tooling, effects runtime, verification (simulation), performance budgets, process

## 1. Summary

LLP 2000 is right that the whole application is the unit of design, right
that ownership comes before abstraction, and right to ask for deterministic
simulation and an early real deployment. Its weakness is that it treats the
two structural choices that produced Snapback 2's costs as open research
questions, and then proposes a first experiment at exactly the scale where
those costs are invisible. This document takes a position on both, names
what to keep from Snapback 2 verbatim, and rewrites the first slice so it
can fail for the right reasons.

The position, in one paragraph. Snapback 2's *core* is excellent and should
be copied: the rule language and its uniformity analysis, the structural-fact
family, one data language, typed states with no silence, refusals that paste
a rewrite, personas, the successor classifier, the derived per-recipient
log, the loop verbs and their sub-second budgets. Two *substrates* under
that core are where the record shows the hours went, and both should be
flipped rather than re-investigated: (1) the static certificate should keep
certifying **authorization and shape** and stop pricing **bytes and steps**,
which move to a deterministic runtime meter with honest `capped`/abort
outcomes; and (2) the client should hold **a replica of the viewer's declared
partition** — the local-first shape every shipped sync engine and Snapback
2's own `deliver` log converge on — instead of a per-query result cache
proven by receipts. Those two flips dissolve most of the machinery that
leaked into the application (receipts, coverage, halos, validators, grants,
ID slices, drains, `withHistory`, follower inboxes, per-table retention
words) and turn the 28-second acquisition into a bulk stream whose floor is
the device's write speed. The first slice should be built on the **hub
fixture** (1,000 memberships, 500 follows, 100,000 messages), with the
day-two change designed before the day-one screen, a native client in the
first slice, and a deterministic simulator as the primary test harness.

Three claims this document makes that LLP 2000 does not:

1. **The costs have two named causes, and both are decisions, not
   mysteries.** Byte-priced static certification and the receipt-proven
   result cache account for every ergonomic residual in LLP 1018.008.009
   except documentation. §2 shows the mapping.
2. **The first experiment must include the hub.** Snapback 2 was "elegant on
   day one and hostile on day two" (LLP 1020) and broke at 1,000 memberships
   (LLPs 1023, 1024, 1025). A two-principal slice will pass and teach nothing.
3. **The process is part of the product.** Seventy-one LLPs, over two
   thousand commits, and seven ceiling raises in fourteen days produced a
   sound system nobody can hold in their head. Snapback 3 needs one owner on
   the core, a simulator instead of a test matrix, and budgets anchored to
   competitors so that raising one means admitting a loss.

## 2. What the record says, read as causes

The evidence LLP 2000 §2 cites is right; its reading is too gentle. Every
row below is a Snapback 2 cost recorded in the corpus, with the substrate
that produced it. Numbers are the corpus's own and retain its scope limits.

| Recorded cost | Where | Cause |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Adding `editedAt: time ?` (44 bytes × 244 retained copies = 10,736) pushed `messages.searchAll` to 4,199,688 closure bytes against 4,194,304; the builder shrank a page from 20 to 19 | LLP 1018.008.008; 0.0.21 day-two diary | **Static byte pricing.** Closure bytes are a bound on what a receipt could ship; the product paid for the bound, not the work |
| The 1,000-membership digest fold certifies at 9,922,698 heap and 8,691,704 closure bytes and is refused; the recipient-keyed form is admitted at 2,733,253 / 1,848,418 | LLP 1025 §3 | **Static byte pricing** (heap = steps × 32 + closure scratch). A runtime meter would have run the fold and reported actual bytes |
| A message page is 50, not 200, because a row "prices at 16,016 bytes" and 200 exceed the 1 MiB output ceiling | LLP 1012 §3 | **Static byte pricing** of the worst-case row width |
| A per-recipient search projection is refused at 20,863,641 reserved steps against 1,000,000 | LLP 1024 §1 | **Static step pricing** of a derivation the engine would perform incrementally |
| Full acquisition of 10,008 messages: 67.4 s → 53.6 s → 32.6 s → 28.4 s against a 2–3 s objective; 201 requests, 200 acknowledgement mutations, 612 IndexedDB transactions; PowerSync reports 10,000 rows in 0.36 s native and 2.5 s on web | LLP 1018.008.007; LLP 1018.008.009 §4 | **Result cache, not replica.** Every 50-row page is a certified query, a receipt, a durable acknowledgement mutation, and a store swap |
| Adding a count to a thread makes the composite `partial` offline whenever count authority is missing, so the template declares an independent `history` query and `withHistory` bindings; 27 files / 1,718 lines → 28 / 1,751 after the fix | LLP 1018.008 F2; LLP 1018.008.009 §2 | **Result cache.** The count is not a fact the receipt covers, so the client cannot compose locally |
| `shared/thread.ts` describes table identity, row selection, order, create membership, draft fields and mutation targets a second time | LLP 1018.008 F2 | **Result cache.** Items must be reconstructed from answers because there is no local table to read |
| Sending stalled with 85 IDs remaining against a certified 352-ID slice on an 8,192-ID grant; recovery needed a "Sync now" action | LLP 1018.008 F1 | **Result cache + engine-minted IDs.** Prediction needs server ids before commit, so ids are granted in slices |
| A follower browser tab writes through a durable inbox row; the SDK carries `forwarding`, `writer-unresponsive`, `inbox-full`, `follower` states | LLP 1018.004 | **Result cache.** One writer per partition image; other tabs cannot mutate a receipt-proven image |
| Global search over 1,000 memberships costs ~1,010 HTTP requests through a client N-way merge | LLP 1024 §2 | **Result cache** (no local index over the partition) + **static pricing** (the server-side projection is refused) |
| The complete local runtime grew 7.8 KB → 89,843 gzip bytes; the ceiling was raised 34,000 → 50,000 → 66,000 → 82,000 → 82,500 → 84,000 → 85,000 → 90,000 | LLP 1000 §"Revised"; `rules/NOT-DOING.md` §Moves; LLP 1018.008 §10 | **Result cache.** The inputs list of `metrics/twin.jsonl` is coverage spans, extent facts, held directories, posting trees, fact integrity, swap journals — receipt machinery, not the interpreter |
| `Snapshot` carries `data, next, partial, receipt{state, source, seq, next, reason, rule, sites}, freshness{live, staleSince, connection}, local{predicted, retained, caughtUp, watermark}, refusal, status, reason` | LLP 1018.003 §1 | Both. Every field is a true fact about the mechanism; almost none is a fact about the application |
| `Schema::rule_hash` binds every queued write to the complete schema; an optional column on the subject table invalidates unrelated evidence | LLP 1018.002; LLP 1018.008.008 | **Result cache.** Evidence identity exists because held answers must be re-authorized; a replica re-runs the mutation at the server and needs no evidence identity |

Against that, what scored: on the 2026-09-05 three-scorer Clique run
Snapback 2 beat Convex on authorization (82 vs 79) and offline (81 vs 44)
and lost on queries and live (60 vs 85), client integration (59 vs 79),
setup and docs (55 vs 79), diagnostics and loop (66 vs 77), operations (53
vs 76) and scale (62 vs 80); overall 66 vs 81. The later single-lane 0.0.21
and 0.0.25 scores of 82 and 85 are real improvement and not a three-scorer
comparison. The wins are the core; the losses are composition, discovery,
scale and operations — the substrates and what sits on them.

LLP 2000 §2 summarises this as "costs arise where one layer must reconstruct
another layer's facts." True, and the layer doing the reconstructing is the
client, because it was designed as a cache that may not compute. Name the
cause and the remedy follows.

## 3. Where LLP 2000 is right, and should not be diluted

- **The whole application is the unit.** §3's four properties (composition,
  locality, continuity, performance) are the right acceptance vocabulary.
  Keep them as the headings of the scenario suite.
- **Ownership before abstraction.** §5's table is the best paragraph in the
  document. §8 below turns its fourth row into a fixed vocabulary.
- **Composed reads must not fabricate.** Unknown totals stay unknown; revoked
  rows do not linger. Both flips below preserve this; they change what is
  *known*, not what is *claimed*.
- **Writes have one lifecycle with durable admission distinct from commit
  and stable operation identity.** Keep LLP 1018.004's three outcomes.
- **Rust and SQLite.** Yes, and the engine's transaction, interpreter, rule
  evaluation and successor logic are the pieces to copy first (§5).
- **Prototype the experience before the core.** Yes — and §8 makes the
  prototype's API signatures binding on the implementation, not the reverse.

The 0.4.33 Mac gate now watches AC power, the lid and sleep during the run, and
restarts an attempt it marks environment-invalid. Details:
[reports/20261017-publication-0.4.31/REPORT.md](reports/20261017-publication-0.4.31/REPORT.md).

Integration 18 upgrade correction: retained executable identity ignores the
compiler-generated device compatibility report, whose server version changes
on a CLI upgrade. The full control-request digest still covers that report;
activation recompiles/rechecks the unchanged retained source. An actual 0.4.33
store with declared privacy intent and rollback history exposed this mismatch.
The focused unit regression distinguishes identity from integrity and preserves
source/native/worker byte changes as new executable content. Proof hosts also
honor their explicit binary environment so qualification cannot accidentally
select a stale checkout release binary.

## 2026-10-19 — SEC4 independent intent and default verification

LLP 3000.036.004 §13/13.5 has Muse and fresh Codex READY design reviews.
SEC4 replaces taught rule copies with spec action/role intent, advises equivalent
invariants, synthesizes typed per-role initial fixtures, labels generated origins,
and adds update-only column intent. Check has a compact note block and bounded
concrete work; test/audit retain full campaigns. Source evidence is read-only;
per-lane measurements and qualification are in reports/20261019-sec4.
DX4 owns staging/upload/dev papercuts; QRY owns read return and hidden lookups.

## 2026-10-19 — SEC4 wave-19 correction

Merged origin/main before changes. Coordinator wave-19 addendum supersedes the
seed-only demotion: authored fixture read/integrity violations fail. Empty
fixtures generate; payload initialization executes authored creators, preserving
requires, principal-role checks and copied values. Exact-source/fixture/CLI
positive evidence carries test refutations into bounded check without cached
proof. Escalation notes bypass the summary cap, exposure counts are separate,
and rule-changing deployment prints a matching-campaign demand. Real role and
relationship transitions retain their committed history. LLP 3000.036.004 §13.7
and wave-19 evidence/report record reviews, qualification and remaining gaps.
Column read rules remain outside SEC4; release visibility belongs to its owner.

Wave-19 follow-up: distinct equal-length histories exposed both real linkUnit
and grantRole → read paths. Counterfactual column mutation must not break an
immutable creator copy; actual typed payloads remain observable. Independent
review reproduced source-change-during-test and followed-symlink fingerprint
bugs. Tests now consume captured source strings and private fixture snapshots,
including symlink target bytes; changed originals prevent persistence. Those
exact regressions pass. Optional role columns retain two active actors per role.

Wave19 independent implementation review also reproduced a post-insert require
through the returned row alias. Paired column independence now follows alias
dependencies into require/check/if guards and declines unresolved coupling;
the exact safe transaction has a regression. Saved refutation records deduplicate
initial observations while retaining complete supporting rows, including the
600-row regression. Packed policy qualification counts actual native or HTTP
challenges separately and retains the existing ephemeral-fork coverage gap.

Packed social follow-up supersedes the earlier ephemeral-fork limitation:
fixture copies now retain independent hub rows/indexes, deadlines, quota
accounting/limits and epoch/ring state under the hub lock, without a publisher.
A mismatched store generation refuses. Durable campaigns run in mixed schemas;
ephemeral mutation histories and external presence are still explicit gaps.
The core regression verifies copied rows, deadlines, global capacity, independent
delete/write/sweep, unchanged live publisher and generation mismatch refusal.
The exact social starter also has a native campaign regression.

/// The store in memory: rows by (table, id) and index entries by their key
/// bytes, the server's layout without SQLite. For tests of the device path
/// and for a host that keeps a partition on the heap.
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct MemoryRows {
    sequences: std::cell::RefCell<std::collections::BTreeMap<(String, String), i64>>,
    rows: std::cell::RefCell<std::collections::BTreeMap<(String, String), Value>>,
    entries: std::cell::RefCell<std::collections::BTreeMap<(String, Vec<u8>), String>>,
}

impl MemoryRows {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — isolated campaign hubs copy rows and index/sequence state together.
    pub(crate) fn fork_fixture(&self)->Self {
        Self{sequences:self.sequences.clone(),rows:self.rows.clone(),entries:self.entries.clone()}
    }
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Every row of a table, by id.
    pub fn rows(&self, table: &str) -> Vec<Value> {
        self.rows.borrow().iter().filter(|((t, _), _)| t == table).map(|(_, row)| row.clone()).collect()
    }

    fn range(&self, table: &str, index_name: &str, lo: &[u8], hi: &[u8], dir: Dir, limit: usize) -> Vec<(Value, Vec<u8>)> {
        let ti = format!("{table}.{index_name}");
        let entries = self.entries.borrow();
        let rows = self.rows.borrow();
        let within = entries.range((ti.clone(), lo.to_vec())..(ti, hi.to_vec()));
        let picked: Vec<(Value, Vec<u8>)> = match dir {
            Dir::Asc => within.take(limit).filter_map(|((_, k), id)| {
                    rows.get(&(table.to_owned(), id.clone())).map(|row| (row.clone(), k.clone()))
                }).collect(),
            Dir::Desc => within.rev().take(limit).filter_map(|((_, k), id)| {

//! Exercise actual logical lifetime reads between deadlines and cleanup ticks.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — no snapshot-only clock refutation.
use super::*;

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock)
        && sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        let policy=inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap();
        if policy.has_reference(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: temporal reference policy needs authorized per-tick engine replay",inv.table));continue;}
        if !kernel::dependencies(policy).is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: clock policy with raw-probe dependencies needs a live-snapshot evaluator",inv.table));continue;}
        let name="$security.temporal";
        let probe=program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"temporal security".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),
            key:vec![Expr::Literal(id(&inv.table))],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))]);
        for variant in [0,4] {
            if !out.reserve(1){return;}
            let rows=match snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,true){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
            for now in [NOW,NOW+999,NOW+1000,NOW+1001,NOW+1999,NOW+2000,NOW+2001] {
                if !out.reserve(1){return;}
                // Recreate the store for each boundary; never sweep before the
                // read. Unswept expiry remains a logical absence by contract.
                let mut engine=match engine(bound,&rows,probe.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                let actual=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                let Some(row)=actual.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())else{continue;};
                for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER] {
                    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    let Ok(q)=engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(now))else{continue;};
                    if q.unavailable||!q.complete||q.data==Value::Null{continue;}
                    // Scalar clock atoms are evaluated only after a real
                    // engine read returned this committed row. This extra
                    // field is proof-private and never enters the store.
                    let mut image=row.clone();
                    if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image {fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(now));}
                    if matches!(rules::evaluate(policy,viewer,None,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows:&actual}),Ok(false)) {
                        out.admitted+=1;
                        out.violations.push(Counterexample {reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:name.into(),
                            effect:"read".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:actual.clone(),rule_path:format!("server read at {now}, before any physical sweep; clock policy false"),
                            scope:"actual engine read over schema-valid committed seed ingress at the recorded server clock; schedules are not assumed to have run".into()});
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

fn fixture_free_first_caller_role_claim_has_an_actual_empty_start_trace(){
    let source="table roles:\n  principal: principal\n  role: enum('staff','guest')\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- .principal = viewer\n  online only\ntable secrets:\n  read <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'staff']\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation register(role: text 1..20):\n  require role = 'staff' or role = 'guest' else BAD_ROLE\n  return insert roles {principal: viewer,role}\ninvariant roles.role write only viewer = 'service:operator'\n";
    let b=bound(source);let mut c=Campaign::default();challenges::generated_campaign(&b,&mut c,false);
    assert!(c.violations.iter().any(|v|v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().any(|s|s.operation=="register")&&v.supporting_rows.values().all(Vec::is_empty)),"{c:?}");
}

#[test]
fn rejected_fallback_final_exists_world_never_becomes_a_failing_fixture(){
    let source="table roots:\n  creator: principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  after insert <- exists members[root = .id]\n  online only\ntable members:\n  root: roots\n  proof: image <=1\n  unique root\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant roots.read only deny\nquery roots():\n  return roots first 100 by .id\n";
    let b=bound(source);let mut c=Campaign::default();
    assert!(challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).is_err(),"invalid final-exists seed must remain unsupported");
    challenges::generated_campaign(&b,&mut c,false);
    assert!(c.violations.is_empty(),"{c:?}");
    assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("final-exists")),"{c:?}");
}

#[test]
fn bounded_campaign_charges_initial_worlds_and_every_mutation_step(){
    let b=bound("table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  body: text <=80\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nmutation add():\n  return insert notes {owner: viewer,body: 'x'}\ninvariant notes.read only .owner = viewer\nquery inspect():\n  return notes first 100 by .id\n");
    let mut c=Campaign::bounded(10000);c.work_remaining=Some(1);
    let fixture=challenges::generated_fixture(&b,&mut c).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(c.work_remaining,Some(0));
    challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&fixture,&mut c);
    assert_eq!(c.attempted,0);assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("work budget exhausted")));
    let fixture=snapback4_core::Engine::memory(b.schema.clone(),b.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
    let mut c=Campaign::bounded(10000);c.work_remaining=Some(1);
    challenges::fixture_observers(&b,&fixture,&mut c);
    assert_eq!(c.attempted,0,"the one reservation pays for the replay world, leaving no mutation step");
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — qualify the installed policy surface and its finite campaigns.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { copyFileSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { runToolchainCommand } from "./publication-starters.mjs";

const sha256 = path => createHash("sha256").update(readFileSync(path)).digest("hex");

/** Execute only the supplied packed client/CLI; never build or repack source.
 * Evidence contains counters and verdicts, never credentials or HTTP bodies. */
export async function proveSecurity(clientTarball, platformTarball, directory) {
  const client = resolve(clientTarball), platform = resolve(platformTarball);
  const artifacts = { clientSha256: sha256(client), platformSha256: sha256(platform) };
  const parent = resolve(directory); mkdirSync(parent, { recursive: true });
  const cwd = mkdtempSync(join(parent, "security-policy-"));
  const env = { ...process.env, CI: "1", npm_config_audit: "false", npm_config_fund: "false" };
  delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN; delete env.PORT_LO; delete env.PORT_HI;
  writeFileSync(join(cwd, "package.json"), '{"private":true,"type":"module"}');
  await runToolchainCommand("npm", ["install", "--ignore-scripts", "--omit=optional", "--no-audit", "--no-fund", client, platform], {
    cwd, env, timeout: 300_000,
  });
  const cli = join(cwd, "node_modules/snapback4/bin/snapback4.js");
  const native = join(cwd, "node_modules", `snapback4-${process.platform}-${process.arch}`, "snapback4");
  const binarySha256 = sha256(native), commands = [];
  function command(args, project = cwd, expected = 0) {
    const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, ...args], {
      cwd: project, env, encoding: "utf8", timeout: 120_000, maxBuffer: 32 * 1024 * 1024,
    });
    commands.push({ args, project: project.slice(cwd.length) || ".", exitCode: result.status, expectedExitCode: expected });
    // Child output can contain replayed rows. Keep failure diagnostics to stable
    // codes and process state rather than serializing potentially private rows.
    const codes = [...new Set(((result.stdout ?? "") + (result.stderr ?? "")).match(/\b[EN]_[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\b/g) ?? [])];
    assert.equal(result.error, undefined, `${args.join(" ")}: ${result.error?.code ?? "process error"}`);
    assert.equal(result.status, expected, `${args.join(" ")}: expected exit ${expected}, got ${result.status}; codes: ${codes.join(", ")}`);
    return result.stdout;
  }
  function schemaProject(name, source) {
    const project = join(cwd, name); mkdirSync(join(project, "snapback"), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(join(project, "snapback/schema.q"), source); return project;
  }
  function checked(project) {
    const result = JSON.parse(command(["check", "--json"], project));
    assert.equal(result.ok, true); assert.ok(result.security?.invariants.length > 0);
    assert.ok(result.security.invariants.every(verdict => ["proved", "unproved"].includes(verdict.status)));
    return result.security;
  }
  function campaign(project, requireHttp = false) {
    const output = command(["test", "--fixture-only"], project);
    const counts = /security tests: (\d+) attempted, (\d+) refused, (\d+) reachable violations; (\d+) seed\/synthetic observations \(non-failing\); finite campaign, not proof/.exec(output);
    assert.ok(counts, "default test executes the generated finite security campaign");
    assert.equal(Number(counts[3]), 0);
    assert.doesNotMatch(output, /security boundary failure:|security HTTP failure:|security violation:/);
    const http = /security unauthenticated serve HTTP: (\d+) attempts refused/.exec(output);
    const artifactBoundary = "security not covered: unauthenticated serve HTTP for native/action/effect applications needs its complete deployed artifacts";
    assert.ok(Number(counts[1]) + Number(http?.[1] ?? 0) > 0, `security campaign attempts policy or isolated HTTP challenges in ${project}`);
    if (requireHttp) assert.ok(http && Number(http[1]) > 0, "the isolated production HTTP campaign runs");
    else assert.ok((http && Number(http[1]) > 0) || output.includes(artifactBoundary), "HTTP coverage is executed or explicitly bounded by deployed artifacts");
    return { attempted: Number(counts[1]), refused: Number(counts[2]), violations: Number(counts[3]), seedSyntheticObservations: Number(counts[4]), unauthenticatedHttpRefused: Number(http?.[1] ?? 0),
      ...(http ? {} : { unauthenticatedHttpNotCovered: "complete deployed native/action/effect artifacts required" }),
      revocationHttpRefused: Number(/security live-session revocation HTTP: (\d+) attempts refused/.exec(output)?.[1] ?? 0) };
  }

  const guide = command(["guide", "security", "--full"]);
  const blocks = [...guide.matchAll(/```quarry\n([\s\S]*?)\n```/g)].map(match => match[1]);
  assert.ok(blocks.length > 0, "installed security guide contains executable Quarry");
  const guideCases = [];
  for (const [index, source] of blocks.entries()) {
    const project = schemaProject(`card-${index}`, source), security = checked(project);
    assert.ok(security.invariants.every(verdict => verdict.direct_rule === "proved"));
    assert.match(readFileSync(join(project, "snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft"), "utf8"), /DRAFT: NOT ACCEPTED/);
    const audited = JSON.parse(command(["audit", "--json"], project));
    assert.equal(audited.format, 1); assert.ok(audited.exposures.some(exposure => exposure.table === "privateNotes"));
    assert.ok(audited.exposures.some(exposure => exposure.table === "privateNotes" && exposure.column_audiences?.body), "installed audit exposes the body column audience");
    const checkAudit = JSON.parse(command(["check", "--audit", "--json"], project));
    assert.equal(checkAudit.format, 1); assert.deepEqual(checkAudit, audited, "check --audit is the structured audit alias");
    const finite = campaign(project, true);
    const broad = source.replace("read <- .patient = viewer", "read <- allow");
    assert.notEqual(broad, source, "the installed card's private read is widened for the counterexample");
    writeFileSync(join(project, "snapback/schema.q"), broad);
    const refuted = JSON.parse(command(["check", "--json"], project, 1));
    assert.equal(refuted.ok, false);
    const readVerdicts = refuted.security.invariants.filter(verdict => verdict.invariant.target === "read");
    assert.equal(readVerdicts.length, 2, "the installed card exercises row and column read intent");
    assert.ok(readVerdicts.every(verdict => verdict.status === "refuted" && ["authored-fixture-trace", "generated-fixture-trace"].includes(verdict.counterexample?.reachability)), "actual authored row/column disclosure retains explicit fixture initialization provenance");
    const auditRefuted = JSON.parse(command(["audit", "--json"], project, 1));
    assert.ok(auditRefuted.invariants.some(verdict => verdict.status === "refuted"));
    command(["test", "--fixture-only"], project, 1);
    guideCases.push({ block: index, invariants: security.invariants.length, audit: "passed", finite, broadRead: "refuted", expectedFailureExits: [1, 1, 1] });
  }

  const editsOnly = schemaProject("column-update-intent", `use identity password

  price: int
  read <- allow
  insert <- allow
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  online only
invariant tickets.price update only viewer = 'service:operator'

  return insert tickets {price: 1}

  checked(editsOnly); campaign(editsOnly, true);
  command(["test"], editsOnly); // No personas, developer store or handwritten fixtures.

  const sessions = [];
  for (const method of ["guests", "password"]) {
    const project = schemaProject(`revocation-${method}`, `use identity ${method}
table notes:
  owner: principal
  read <- .owner = viewer
  insert <- .owner = viewer
  online only
invariant notes.read only .owner = viewer
query notes():
  return notes first 10 by .id

    checked(project);
    const finite = campaign(project, true);
    assert.equal(finite.revocationHttpRefused, 14, "actual refresh and logout-all retire both credentials at all seven doors");
    sessions.push({ method, status: "passed", ...finite, disconnectedDevice: "not covered" });
  }

  const starters = [];
  for (const starter of [
    { id: "todo-backend", flags: [], intent: "invariant todos.toggle only .ownerId = viewer" },
    { id: "todo-online", flags: ["--online"], intent: "invariant todos.toggle only .ownerId = viewer" },
    { id: "social", flags: ["--template", "social"] },
    { id: "todo-web", flags: ["--web"] },
    { id: "chat-web", flags: ["--template", "chat", "--web"], intent: "invariant messages.send only .authorId = viewer" },
    { id: "chat-expo", flags: ["--template", "chat", "--expo"], intent: "invariant messages.send only .authorId = viewer" },
  ]) {
    const project = join(cwd, starter.id); command(["init", project, ...starter.flags]);
    const source = readFileSync(join(project, "snapback/schema.q"), "utf8");
    if (starter.intent) assert.ok(source.includes(starter.intent), `${starter.id} ships its independent intent`);
    const security = checked(project), finite = campaign(project);
    starters.push({ id: starter.id, status: "passed", invariants: security.invariants.length, ...finite });
  }
  const production = schemaProject("production-recipe", "");
  copyFileSync(join(cwd, "node_modules/snapback4/dist/recipes/production/web/schema.q"), join(production, "snapback/schema.q"));
  const productionSecurity = checked(production);
  starters.push({ id: "production-recipe", status: "passed", source: "installed dist/recipes/production/web/schema.q", invariants: productionSecurity.invariants.length, ...campaign(production) });
  assert.equal(starters.length, 7);

  assert.equal(sha256(native), binarySha256, "installed CLI binary is unchanged");
  assert.equal(sha256(client), artifacts.clientSha256, "supplied client tarball is unchanged");
  assert.equal(sha256(platform), artifacts.platformSha256, "supplied platform tarball is unchanged");
  const result = { status: "passed", guide: "verbatim installed security card", artifacts: { ...artifacts, binarySha256 }, guideCases, sessions, starters,
    scope: "finite campaigns and declared policy checks; no zero-hole or disconnected-device revocation claim", commands };
  writeFileSync(join(cwd, "security-evidence.json"), JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) + "\n");
  return result;
}

fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
    for inv in &bound.invariants{
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"read"|"always")||inv.operation.as_ref().is_some_and(|op|op!=&p.name){continue;}
        let judged;
        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
            judged=stable;&judged
        };
        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(old,NOW)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
            }
        }
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(r,NOW));
            if old==Some(row){continue;}
            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
            if !super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
            if inv.target=="always" {
                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
            }else{
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
                }
            }
        }
    }

fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
        match (kind,value) {
            (C::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>hydrate(engine,inner,v),
            (C::List{of,..}|C::ListRange{of,..},Value::Array(xs))=>xs.iter().map(|v|hydrate(engine,of,v)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map(Value::Array),
            (C::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>engine.store_asset(VIEWER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|format!("verified image fixture: {e}")),
            (C::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(engine,VIEWER,types),
            _=>Ok(value.clone()),
        }
    }

fn engine_on_store(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>,store:Store)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let mut programs=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();programs.extend(probes);
    let mut engine=Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),programs,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let mut seeded=rows.iter().filter(|(name,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_none()).map(|(name,rs)|{
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
        let clean=rs.iter().map(|r|if let Value::Row(fields)=r {Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect())}else{r.clone()}).collect();
        (name.clone(),clean)
    }).collect::<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>();
    for (table,values) in &mut seeded {for row in values {for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns {
        if let Ok(v)=row.field(column) {
            let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,v)?;
            if let Value::Row(fields)=row {fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}
        }
    }}}
    engine.seed_at(seeded.iter().flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>(),NOW).map_err(|e|format!("generated snapshot rejected by schema: {e}"))?;
    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
    Ok(engine)
}
fn validate_fixture(bound:&Bound,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<(),String>{
    let actual=readback(engine)?;
    for (name,values) in &actual {for row in values {
        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&bound.schema,name,row).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        for c in &bound.schema.tables[name].constraints {if let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=c {
            let rule=RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),key:key.clone(),provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities:Vec::new()};
            if engine.fixture_policy(&rule,snapback4_core::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,None,row,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=rules::Verdict::Readable {return Err(format!("{name}: trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"));}
        }}
    }}
    Ok(())
}

/bin/bash -lc "timeout 20s git show c4a21db:snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/security/http.rs | sed -n '168,236p'" in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4

    Ok(attempted)

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)])->Result<usize,String>{
    // Auth admission is exercised on the actual schema. External worker/native
    // startup needs its app-owned artifacts; never pretend this schema-only
    // host qualifies those paths.
    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.native||p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action)
        || !bound.schema.effects.is_empty() || !bound.schema.action_specs.is_empty(){
        println!("security not covered: unauthenticated serve HTTP for native/action/effect applications needs its complete deployed artifacts");return Ok(0);
    }
    let stamp=SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_nanos();
    let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-security-serve-{}-{stamp}",std::process::id()));
    fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;let scratch=Scratch(root);
    fs::create_dir(scratch.0.join("snapback")).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    for (name,text) in sources {
        let file=std::path::Path::new(name).file_name().ok_or("source needs a file name")?;
        fs::write(scratch.0.join("snapback").join(file),text).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    }
    let executable=scratch.0.join("security-host");
    image::snapshot(&executable)?;
    let output=fs::File::create(scratch.0.join("host.log")).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let command=|verb:&str|->Result<Command,String>{
        let mut c=Command::new(&executable);c.arg(verb).current_dir(&scratch.0)
            .env("SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID",std::process::id().to_string()).stdin(Stdio::null())
            .stdout(output.try_clone().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?).stderr(output.try_clone().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?);Ok(c)
    };
    let mut deploy=Process(command("deploy")?.args(["deployment"]).spawn().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?);
    wait(&mut deploy,Duration::from_secs(30))?;drop(deploy);
    let listener=TcpListener::bind((std::net::Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST,0)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    let address=listener.local_addr().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;drop(listener);
    let mut host=Process(command("serve")?.args(["deployment","--bind","127.0.0.1","--port",&address.port().to_string()]).spawn().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?);
    let until=Instant::now()+Duration::from_secs(15);
    loop{
        if host.0.try_wait().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_some(){return Err("private production serve exited during startup".into());}
        if TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address,Duration::from_millis(100)).is_ok(){break;}
        if Instant::now()>=until{return Err("private production serve did not listen within 15 seconds".into());}
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
    }
    let mut attempted=0;
    for p in &bound.programs {
        let prefix=match p.program.kind{snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Query=>"q",snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation=>"m",_=>continue};
        for forged in [false,true]{
            let (status,body)=request(address,&format!("/{prefix}/{}",p.program.name),forged)?;attempted+=1;
            let ephemeral=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation&&bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral);
            if !auth_refusal(status,&body,ephemeral) {
                return Err(format!("unauthenticated serve HTTP {} (forged persona={forged}) returned {status}: {body}",p.program.name));
            }
        }
    }
    // Staging/provisioning belongs to STAGE. This host never opens its dev doors.
    for path in ["/seed","/dev/reset","/dev/clock/advance"] {
        let (status,_)=request(address,path,false)?;attempted+=1;
        if !matches!(status,401|403|404){return Err(format!("production development door {path} returned {status}"));}
    }
    let revoked=revoked_sessions(bound,address)?;
    drop(host);drop(scratch);
    println!("security unauthenticated serve HTTP: {attempted} attempts refused (including forged personas and dev doors)");
    Ok(attempted+revoked)
}

//! Isolated complete fixtures, including captured authority and asset ownership.
use super::*;
impl Store {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — fork committed state, never reinterpret stored rows as new seed input.
    pub fn fork_memory(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let mut fork = Self::memory()?;
        {
            let backup = rusqlite::backup::Backup::new(&self.conn, &mut fork.conn)?;
            backup.run_to_completion(256, std::time::Duration::ZERO, None)?;
        }
        fork.catalog = self.catalog.clone();
        fork.identity = self.identity.clone();
        fork.server = self.server;
        if self.assets_dir.exists() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fork.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)? {
                let entry = entry.map_err(io)?;
                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file() {
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset ledger contains a non-file".into()));
                }
                std::fs::copy(entry.path(), fork.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(fork)
    }
}

fn io(e:std::io::Error)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string())}

impl Store {
    /// Independent valid worlds share verified bytes, never source row state.
    pub fn empty_with_asset_ledger(&self)->Result<Self,RuntimeError>{
        let fresh=Self::memory()?;
        let ids=self.conn.prepare("SELECT id FROM _assets")?.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
        for id in ids{
            let mut asset=self.asset(&id)?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset disappeared".into()))?;
            asset.home=None;asset.released_at=None;
            fresh.record_asset(&asset)?;
        }
        if self.assets_dir.exists(){
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fresh.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)?{
                let entry=entry.map_err(io)?;
                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file(){return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset is not a regular file".into()));}
                std::fs::copy(entry.path(),fresh.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(fresh)
    }
}

    pub fn mutate(&mut self, name: &str, op: Option<&str>, mut context: Context) -> Result<MutationOutcome, RuntimeError> {
        context.generation = self.generation;
        let requested = context.now.unwrap_or_else(crate::now_ms);
        context.now = Some(self.advance_time_audience(requested)?);
        let wire_args = if op.is_some() { context.wire_args.take().unwrap_or_else(|| {
                serde_json::Value::Object(crate::wire::map_to_json(&context.args))
            }) } else { serde_json::Value::Null };
        if let Some(op) = op
            && let Some(outcome) = self.replay_mutation(&context.viewer, op, name, &wire_args)?
        {
            return Ok(outcome);
        }
        // An existing durable receipt owns its id even after conversion to ephemeral.
        if self.schema.operations.get(name).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral) {
            self.require_non_media_ephemeral(&context.viewer,op)?;
            self.schema.check_delivery(name, context.delivery_fingerprint.as_deref())?;
            let viewer=context.viewer.clone();
            return match self.mutate_ephemeral(name, context) {
                Ok(out) => Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Sent { seq: out.seq, result: out.result }, metered: out.metered, tables: out.ephemeral.tables, events: 0, replayed: false }),
                Err(error) if error.is_refusal() => Ok(MutationOutcome { receipt_only: false, state: WriteState::Failed { why: seat_refusal(&self.schema,&error,&viewer), current: None }, metered: Metered::default(), tables: Vec::new(), events: 0, replayed: false }),
                Err(error) => Err(error),
            };
        }
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        if let Some(op) = op
            && let Some(outcome) = crate::store::replay_bound(tx.conn(), &context.viewer, op, name, &wire_args)?
        {
            // Applied between the check above and this transaction.
            let outcome = MutationOutcome::from_receipt(&outcome)?;
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(outcome);
        }
        if let Some(op)=op{assets::require_placement(&tx,&context.viewer,op,name,&wire_args)?;}

//! Real upload ledgers for foreign-id and non-image placement challenges.
// @ref LLP 3000.038#3-upload — assets retain upload ownership and verified media type.
use super::*;
const PDF:&[u8]=b"%PDF-1.4\n%%EOF\n";
pub(super) fn file(e:&mut Engine,owner:&str,types:&[String])->Result<Value,String>{
    let mime=types.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("application/pdf");
    let (bytes,name)=match mime{"application/pdf"=>(PDF,"proof.pdf"),"text/plain"=>(b"verified text\n".as_slice(),"proof.txt"),"image/png"=>(PNG.as_slice(),"proof.png"),_=>return Err(format!("file ledger needs a verified {mime} fixture"))};
    e.store_file(owner,bytes,Some(mime),name,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn contains(value:&Value,id:&Value)->bool {
    if value==id{return true;}
    match value{Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Map(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Row(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    if initial.values().any(|xs|!xs.is_empty()){
        out.unsupported.insert("foreign-asset replay over populated fixtures needs full ledger transfer; empty-ledger challenges run separately".into());
    }
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let p=&bp.program;
        if ephemeral_operation(bound,p,out){continue;}
        for (arg,kind) in &p.args{
            if !matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}|ir::ArgType::File{..}){continue;}
            for mode in ["foreign upload","non-image photo"]{
                if mode=="non-image photo"&&!matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}){continue;}
                let mut e=match engine(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),program("$security.asset",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let sample=if mode=="non-image photo"{file(&mut e,VIEWER,&[])}else{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Image{..}=>e.store_asset(OTHER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
                    ir::ArgType::File{types,..}=>file(&mut e,OTHER,types),_=>unreachable!(),
                }};
                let sample=match sample{Ok(v)=>v,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let Some(mut args)=arguments(&p.args,0)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge needs remaining argument fixtures",p.name));continue;};
                args.insert(arg.clone(),sample.clone());
                out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                match e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(VIEWER,args).at(NOW)){
                    Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>out.refused+=1,
                    Ok(_)=>{if let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)&&rows.values().flatten().any(|row|contains(row,&sample)){
                        out.boundary_failures.push(format!("{} placed {mode} through argument {arg}",p.name));
                    }},
                    Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge engine error {error}",p.name));},
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
